個股報告

台達電(2308) 
缺料與物料問題，創造長線布局機會 
投資評等 目標價 
逢低買進 $2,000 
前次投資建議：2026.07.30 
「買進」，目標價: $2,000

報告日期：2026.09.16 研 究 員：邱岳霖

摘 要 及 調 整 原 因 
➢ 缺料影響營運不如預期，下修 Q3獲利預估，EPS 9元 
➢ HVDC滲透率提升，且持續海內外擴產，2027 年營收挑戰超越 1兆元，EPS 62.3元。考量
電源產業將進入全新格局且台達作為主要先行者將最為受惠，兆豐投顧維持目標價 2000 元
(約 32X 27(F)PER)，潛在上漲空間 19.8%，建議逢低買進。

基本資料 結論與建議 
2026/9/15 收盤價： 1670.00 ➢ 台達電 (2308)公告 8月營收：營收 645.9億元， MoM 
-3.7%/YoY +34.9%，Q3達成率62.4%，低於研究部預
期與市場共識，係因 MOSFET全產品線呈現缺料所
致，故研究部下修台達電 26Q3營收1960.8億元(原估
2,107.4億元 )，QoQ +7.0%/YoY +30.4% ，而先前
18.3KW PSU因電容問題有進行產品召回，不過預期本
月將處理完畢且對財務無任何影響；毛利率方面，受惠
產品組合轉佳與規模經濟抵銷原料 /電子元件漲價影
響，預估毛利率 35.8%，仍可維持與上季相當，季增
0.2ppt，反映營收下修，同步下修稅後淨利232.6億元，
QoQ -7.5%/YoY +25.0%，EPS 9元(原估10.5元) ，
2026年稅後淨利960.8億元，YoY +59.8%，EPS 37.0
元（原估40元） 。 
➢ 展望2027年，受惠CSP業者持續加大投資力道，台達
於國內外同步擴產，研究部認為HVDC較大規模量產貢
獻有望於2027年起顯現，同時液冷、電源機櫃、DC-DC
模組等新產品線持續放量，此外公司亦持續推進 SST
與SOFC等能源基礎設施長期布局，電源產業將迎來新
格局與長期成長動能。兆豐投顧預估 台達電2027年營
收1.1兆元，，YoY +43.0%，毛利率37%，年增0.8ppt，

- 2 - 
個股報告 
資料來源：CMoney；兆豐投顧預估

營 運 分 析

台達電為全球電源管理及散熱解決方案領導廠商 
台達電(2308)成立於 1971 年並於 1988 年掛牌上市，成立初期分別投入生產
交換式電源供應器與無碳刷式直流風扇，目 前已取得全球電源產品龍頭地
位。2017 年公司啟動組織調整，以「電源及零組件」、「自動化」、「基礎
設施」、「交通」四大業務範疇，並從傳統關鍵零組件製造商逐步轉型為節
能解決方案提供者。近年隨AI 資料中心建置需求爆發，公司積極將既有電源
技術延伸至 AI伺服器電源機櫃、液冷 side car、BBU、DC-DC/AC-DC模組
及 CDU 等新產品線，目前終端應用已從傳統 NB/工業電源、資料中心跨足
至高壓供電(HVDC)與液冷散熱系統，客戶涵蓋NVIDIA與北美 CSP及 ASIC
供應鏈。

26Q2 營收比重:(1)電源及零組件 53%,包含電源、被動元件等零組件、風扇
散熱;(2)交通 5%,電動車解決方案;(3)自動化 8%,包含工業自動化,樓宇自動
化;(4)基礎設施 34%,包含資訊通訊技術(通信電源、網通交換器、資料中心
解決方案、液冷side car)、能源基礎設施(電動車充電樁、可再生能源及儲能
設備等)。受惠 AI 資料中心電源需求與規格持續提升，台達持續擴充台灣、
中國大陸、泰國與美國產能，並同步推進 800V HVDC 架構開發，全面迎接
NVIDIA 新世代 GPU 機櫃功耗大幅提升所帶動的電源與散熱升級需求。

圖一、台達各部門營收比重

- 3 - 
個股報告

缺料影響營運不如預期，下修 Q3獲利預估，EPS 9 元 
台達電(2308)公告 8月營收：營收 645.9 億元，MoM -3.7%/YoY +34.9%，
Q3達成率 62.4%，低於研究部預期與市場共識，係因 MOSFET 全產品線呈
現缺料所致，故研究部下修台達電 26Q3 營收 1960.8 億元(原估 2,107.4 億
元)，QoQ +7.0%/YoY +30.4%，而先前 18.3KW PSU 因電容問題有進行產
品召回，不過預期本月將處理完畢且對財務無任何影響；毛利率方面，受惠
產品組合轉佳與規模經濟抵銷原料/電子元件漲價影響，預估毛利率 35.8%，
仍可維持與上季相當，季增 0.2ppt，反映營收下修，同步下修稅後淨利 232.6
億元，QoQ -7.5%/YoY +25.0%，EPS 9元(原估 10.5 元)。

AI 電源與散熱產品持續放量，輔以新產品推出，2026 EPS 37元 
展望 2026 年，主要成長動能仍來自 AI 資料中心電源、散熱與基礎設施，在
電源方面隨著 GB300 持續放量與 H2 VR200之 18.3KW 產品開始出貨，預
期價量齊揚挹注營運動能，此外 HVDC(±400V/800V)產品於 3Q26 開始量
產，惟在初期階段預期僅小幅貢獻；在散熱產品方面(涵蓋水冷板、Manifold、
CDU及 Sidecar)在 2026 下半年可望延續成長動能，主要受惠液冷滲透率持
續提升與內容價值同步墊高。綜上所述，反映 Q3下修，同步下修台達電 2026
年營運預估：2026 年營收 7,465.4 億元，YoY +34.5%，毛利率 36.2%，年
增 1.9ppt，稅後淨利 960.8 億元，YoY +59.8%，EPS 37.0元（原估 40 元）。

HVDC滲透率提升，且持續海內外擴產，2027年營收挑戰超越 1兆元，EPS 
62.3元 
展望 2027 年，受惠 CSP業者 CAPEX 持續增長，公司為滿足訂單需求，持
續加大投資力道，2026 年資本支出約 700 億元(YoY +50%)，資金來源除自
有資金外，9/15 公告發行泰達電可交換公司債籌措 15 億美元以滿足擴產資
金需求，2027 年也可望維持高檔水準，預計於泰國、中國、台灣與美國同步
擴產，研究部認為 HVDC(±400V/800V)較大規模量產貢獻有望於 2027 年起
顯現，同時液冷、電源機櫃、DC-DC模組等新產品線持續放量，此外公司亦
持續推進 SST 與 SOFC 等能源基礎設施長期布局，電源產業將迎來新格局
與長期成長動能。兆豐投顧預估台達電 2027 年營收 1.1 兆元，YoY +43.0%，
毛利率 37%，年增 0.8ppt，稅後淨利 1617.6 億元，YoY +68.4%，EPS 62.3
元。

評等分級：買進（預期未來六個月股價漲幅超過+30%）; 逢低買進（預期未來六個月股價漲幅介於+15%與+30%之間）; 區間操

## Document 060: 2330台積電 群益

Source PDF: all report pdf/2330台積電 群益.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-09-09
Pages: 3
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

產業速報  
                                        2026 年 9 月 9 日

1 群益投顧 
ASML (ASML US) 與台積電 (2330 TT) 共同推動 High NA EUV 將自現行的 6 吋光罩，
轉移至更大的 12 吋 光 罩。 目標於 2031 年之前建立 12 吋 光 罩 試 產 線(pilot line) ， 並 於
2033 年之前實現微影系統全面就緒。台積電並表示自 2030 年 起 ， 將 在 先 進 製 程 量 產
中導入 ASML 的 High NA  EUV 。台積電與 ASML 共同發起 6 吋光罩轉移至 12 吋光
罩 的 規 格 轉 移 ， 其 戰 略 意 義 在 於 ， 製 訂 規 格 和 標 準 ， 確 保 台 積 電 技 術 領 先 地 位 。 因 此
對 台 積 電 的 投 資 評 等 Buy ，目標價 3,200 元。

研 究 員 ： 陳 俐 妍   
ly.c@capital.com.tw

ASML 與 台 積 電 共 同 推 動 High NA EUV 將 自 現 行 的 6 吋 光 罩 ， 轉 移 至 更 大
的 12 吋 光 罩 ：  
09/07/2026 ASML 與 台 積 電 於 BACUS 國 際 光 學 工 程 學 會(SPIE) 先 進 微 影 技
術 研 討 會 前 宣 布 發 起 產 業 合 作 ， 共 同 推 動High NA EUV 將 自 現 行 的 6 吋光
罩 ， 轉 移 至 更 大 的 12 吋 光 罩 ， 預 期 將 進 一 步 提 升 晶 圓 廠 的 生 產 力 、 降 低 晶
片 製 造 成 本 ， 並 消 除 需 要 2 張 6 吋 光 罩 在 High NA EUV 曝 光 時 進 行 拼 接 的
限 制，使 整 個 半 導 體 產 業 受 惠。此 轉 移 能 讓 先 進 晶 片 製 造 服 務 提 供 者 充 分 利
用 High NA EUV 的 優 勢 ， 更 提 升 未 來 世 代 先 進 晶 片 的 成 本 效 益 。 
目標於 2031 年 之 前 建 立 12 吋 光 罩 試 產 線(pilot line) ，並於 2033 年 之 前 實
現 微 影 系 統 全 面 就 緒 ， 以 支 援 先 進 製 程 量 產 。 台 積 電 並 表 示 自2030 年 起 ，
將 在 先 進 製 程 量 產 中 導 入 ASML 的 High NA 技 術 。  
12 吋光罩 使 High NA 效 率 提 升 ：  
現行 EUV 設備的 NA(Numerical Aperture ， NA) 為 0.33 ， High NA 則 提 高 到
0.55 。 NA 數 值 越 高 ， 解 析 度 也 越 高 、 曝 光 時 能 呈 現 的 細 節 愈 多 。ASML 資
料顯示 High NA 可 將 最 小 解 析 尺 寸 從 現 行 EUV 的約 13 奈 米 進 一 步 縮 小 到
8 奈 米 ， 也 就 是 能 在 晶 圓 上 印 出 更 細 微 的 電 路 圖 形 。 部 分 原 本 需 要2 次 甚 至
更多次曝光才能完成的圖形，也有機會改以單次曝光處理，降低製程複雜
度 、 缺 陷 風 險 與 生 產 周 期 。 
舉 例 來 說 ，0.33 NA EUV 機 台 ， 至 少 需 要 4 張 光 罩 、 分 多 次 曝 光 才 能 完 成
A14 、 A10 金 屬 層 圖 形 ；High NA EUV ， 只 要 1 張 光 罩 就 能 完 成 相 同 圖 形 。
但 解 析 度 提 高 ，High NA 的 光 學 設 計 讓 單 次 可 曝 光 範 圍 縮 小 到 原 本EUV 的
50% ， 製 作 大 顆 晶 片 時 可 能 必 須 分 成2 次 曝 光 ， 再 把 圖 形 拼 接 起 來 。 這 也 是
台積電與 ASML 此 次 推 動 12 吋 光 罩 的 原 因 ， 相 較 現 行 6 吋 光 罩 ， 放 大 光 罩
尺 寸 預 期 能 進 一 步 提 升 晶 圓 廠 生 產 力、降 低 晶 片 製 造 成 本，並 消 除 High NA
曝 光 時 的 拼 接 限 制 。 
台 積 電 預 計 2030 年 開 始 使 用 High NA  EUV ， 推估 High NA 將 會 在 A10 或
A10 以 下 的 先 進 製 程 採 用：  
台積電預計 2030 年 開 始 導入 High NA  EUV ，比 12 吋 光 罩 微 影 系 統 預 計 全
面就緒的 2033 年 更 早 。 這 代 表 台 積 電 初 期 導 入High NA 時 ， 仍 會 先 使 用 現
行 6 吋 光 罩 ， 之 後 再 隨 12 吋 光 罩 與 相 關 系 統 成 熟 逐 步 轉 換 。

### Page 2

產業速報  
                                        2026 年 9 月 9 日

2 群益投顧 
根 據 台 積 電 目 前 公 開 的 製 程 藍 圖 ，A13 與 A12 製 程 都 預 計 2029 年 量 產 。 其
中 A13 是 A14 平 台 的 延 伸 ，A12 則 將 超 級 電 軌(Super Power Rail) 背面供電
技術導入 A14 平 台 。 因 此 N2 、 A16 、 A14 、 A13 與 A12 都 早 於 台 積 電 預 計
2030 年 導 入 High NA ， 推 估 High NA 將 會 在 A10 或 A10 以 下 的 先 進 製 程 採
用。  
Intel (INTC US) 和 Samsung (005930 KS) 領 先 台 積 電 採 用 High NA EUV ，但
也 同 時 參 與 12 吋 光 罩 開 發 ：  
Intel 在 2024 年 宣 布 完 成 全 球 首 套 商 用 High NA EUV 設 備 的 組 裝 ；2026 年
High NA 已 經 正 式 進 入 量 產 ， 並 用 於 部 分 18A 製 程 層 。 是 目 前 High NA 導
入 進 度 最 快 的 晶 片 製 造 商 。Samsung 則預計 2028 年導入 DRAM 量 產 ； 台 積
電選擇在 2030 年 導 入 。Intel 和 Samsung 也 同 時 表 示 與 ASML 及 供 應 鏈 合
作 ， 推 動 未 來 轉 向 大 尺 寸 光 罩 。 
台 積 電 與 ASML 共 同 發 起 6 吋 光 罩 轉 移 至 12 吋 光 罩 的 規 格 轉 移，將 領 導 業
界 規 格 ：  
6 吋 光 罩 轉 移 至 12 吋光罩 的 這 項 升 級 非 單 純 的 尺 寸 放 大 ， 而 是 面 臨 生 態 系
與 物 理 極 限 的 多 重 挑 戰 。 整 體 供 應 鏈 重 構 ， 包 括 基 板 與 材 料 以 及 設 備 等 等 。
12 吋 光 罩 石 英 基 板 的 平 坦 度 、 缺 陷 密 度 與 熱 膨 脹 係 數 控 制 難 度 倍 增 。 製 程
設 備 如 薄 膜 沉 積、蝕 刻、清 洗 與 自 動 化 搬 運 設 備 皆 需 重 新 開 發。後 續 檢 測 與
量測極限檢測難度也增加，更大面積的圖形缺陷檢測（ Inspection ）與修補
技 術 需 要 突 破 。 在 12 吋 面 積 上 維 持 奈 米 級 的 對 位 精 度 與 線 寬 均 勻 性 極 為 困
難   
電 子 設 計 自 動 化 (EDA) 工 具 必 須 重 新 適 應 大 尺 寸 曝 光 視 場 與 資 料 處 理 。 12 
吋 光 罩 的 圖 形 檔 案 與 運 算 複 雜 度 將 大 幅 上 升 。 
台積電與 ASML 共 同 發 起 6 吋 光 罩 轉 移 至 12 吋 光 罩 的 規 格 轉 移，其 戰略意
義在於 ，台 積 電 為 全 球 最 大 晶 圓 代 工 廠，為 先 進 製 程 最 大 的 供 應 商，可 以 領
導 光 罩 基 底 供 應 商 、 光 罩 檢 測 設 備 商， 甚 至 EDA 廠 等 整 個 生 態 系 ，除 共同
分攤 ， 跨 越 升 級 12 吋 光 罩 的 龐 大 研 發 成 本外 ， 製 訂 規 格 和 標 準 ， 確 保 台 積
電 技 術 領 先 地 位 。因 此 對 台 積 電 的投 資 評 等 Buy ， 目 標 價 3,200 元。

### Page 3

產業速報  
                                        2026 年 9 月 9 日

3 群益投顧 
  【 投 資 評 等 說 明 】 
  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15%≤ 首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間<35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy)  5%≤ 首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間<15%

中立 (Neutral)  
無 法 由 基 本 面 給 予 投 資 評 等 
預 期 近 期 股 價 將 處 於 盤 整 
建 議 降 低 持 股

【 投 資 評 等 調 整 機 制 】 
  投 資 評 等 係「 首 次 」給 予 特 定 個 股 投 資 評 等 時，其 前 一 日 收 盤 價 相 較3 個月
目 標 價 之 潛 在 上 漲 空 間 計 算 而 得 。 個 股 投 資 評 等 分 為四 個 等 級 ， 定 義 如 上 。
爾 後 的 投 資 評 等係 依 循 「 首 次 評 等 」 ， 直 到 停 止 推 薦 。  
「 強 力 買 進 」、「 買 進 」及 「 區 間 操 作 」均有 upside 目 標 價 。差 別 僅 在 於 ，
首 次 評 等 時 潛 在 報 酬 率 不 同 。 「 中 立 」 則 無 目 標 價 。 
一 旦 我 們 給 予 特 定 個 股「 強 力 買 進 」、「 買 進 」或「 區 間 操 作 」之 投 資 評 等，
就 是 責 任 的 開 始，爾 後 將 透 過 各 式 研 究 報 告 作 定 期 性、持 續 性 追 蹤 基 本 面 及
股 價 變 化 ， 直 到 停 止 推 薦 。 
停 止 推 薦 情 境 ： 
1.  達 目 標 價 。 
2.  雖 未 達 原 訂 目 標 價 ， 但 檢 視 基 本 面 、 訊 息 面 、 籌 碼 面 等 多 方 訊 息 ， 研
判 股 價 上 漲 空 間 已 然 有 限 ， 將 適 時 出 具 降 評 報 告 。 
3.  推 薦 後 股 價 不 漲 反 跌 。

【免責聲明】  
  本 研 究 報 告 僅 提 供 予 特 定 人 之 客 戶 作 為 參 考 資 料「 非 經 同 意 不 得 轉 載 」 。我
們 並 不 確 保 此 資 訊 的 完 整 性 與 正 確 性，投 資 人 應 了 解，報 告 中 有 關 未 來 預 測
之 陳 述 可 能 不 會 實 現，因 而 不 應 被 依 賴。而 且 此 報 告 並 非 根 據 特 定 投 資 目 的
或 依 預 定 對 象 之 財 務 狀 況 所 撰 寫 出 來 的，因 此，此 研 究 報 告 的 目 的，既 非 對
投 資 人 於 買 賣 證 券、選 擇 權、期 貨 或 其 他 證 券 相 關 之 衍 生 性 商 品 提 供 詢 價 服
務，亦 非 作 為 進 行 交 易 的 要 約。投 資 人 應 注 意 到 相 關 證 券 之 價 值 及 收 益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

2026.09.10 
徐文華 C00215@ibfs.com.tw 
2357.TT 華碩 
 2H26 AI 動能續強

[財務資訊更新]

公司簡介 
華碩電腦成立於 1990 年，以生產個人電腦主機板起家，後來跨入筆記型電腦、無線
網通、及 3C 消費電子產品領域，以自有品牌「ASUS」銷售全球。2006 年以 ROG 
(Republic of Gamers) 品牌發展高階電競主機板及筆電等產品，樹立在電競市場的地
位，近年亦開始發展 AI 伺服器、AIoT 產品，搶攻 AI 商機。 
投資評等/目標價 重點摘要 
買進 1,130 
【 前 次 投 資 建 議 】區 間 操 作，840 
【大盤指數/股價】47183 / 979

➢ 2H26 AI 伺服器強勢成長  
華碩 8 月營收 949.6 億元 ，MoM+12.9%，YoY+51.2%， 公司預期3Q26 
PC 營收 QoQ+15%~20% ， YoY+20%~30% ， 零 組 件 營 收  
QoQ+5%~10%、YoY 持平，伺服器營收 QoQ+10%，YoY+150%。雖
然 PC 產業面臨缺料與關鍵零組件成本上升的挑戰，但記憶體的漲價帶
動 PC 售價上升，加上華碩高價值產品(如:高階電競、Copilot+PC 等)已
占營收 65%以上，此類產品的消費者價格敏感度較低，因此公司認為
3Q26 PC 業務價、量都優於 2Q26。 
華碩 AI 伺服器業務持續強勁 ， 客戶包含CSP、Neo cloud 以及主權 AI，
公司的伺服器營收當中，90%由 AI 貢獻(AI 中 HGX 約占營收 65%、
GB300 約占營收 35%)，預期伺服器 2026 全年營收 YoY+150%。投研
部估計華碩 3Q26 營收 2,773.6 億元，QoQ+7.6%，YoY+38.5%，毛利
率 16.3%，EPS 25.10 元。  
➢ IFA 2026 展出搭載 NVIDIA RTX Spark 的 ProArt PC 
華碩於 IFA 2026 展前發表會中 ， 已發表搭載 RTX Spark 的 ProArt P16、
ProArt P14、ProArt GR1X mini PC：1) ProArt P16 機身厚度 12.9 mm、
重量 1.77kg， 相較前一代薄14%、輕 9%， 為該系列歷來為最輕薄的16
吋版本；2) ProArt P14 機身厚度 13.9 mm、重量 1.48kg，搭載 90Wh
電池提供全天候續航；3) ProArt GR1X 尺寸 150×150×51 mm ，主打
機身僅掌心大小 ，同時兼具強大AI 運算效能 。 華碩表示，ProArt P16 與
ProArt P14 首波已預購一空，新品將於 4Q26 出貨。關於 AI PC 市場，
華碩之 AI PC 2Q26 已在消費型 NB 營收中達到 30%占比，公司認為未
來 2~3 年 AI PC、Edge AI 將引發產品升級循環 ， 對2H26 亦樂觀看待 。 
➢ 建議買進，目標價 1,130 元 
華碩 2H26 在 AI 伺服器與 AI PC 的帶動下展望樂觀，預期 2027 年 AI
伺服器能為公司帶來穩定的成長動能 。 華碩歷史PE 區間為 9X~18X，考
量公司營運展望正向 ， 投研部認為有利帶動其PE 回升 ， 買進目標價1,130
元(2027F PE 13X)。

2H26 AI 伺服器強勢成長 
華碩 8 月營收 949.6 億元，MoM+12.9%，YoY+51.2%，公司預期 3Q26 PC 營收
QoQ+15%~20%、YoY+20%~30%，零組件營收 QoQ+5%~10%、YoY 持平，伺服
器營收 QoQ+10%，YoY+150%。雖然 PC 產業面臨缺料與關鍵零組件成本上升的挑
戰 ， 但 記 憶 體 的 漲 價 帶 動PC 售 價 上 升 ， 加 上 華 碩 高 價 值 產 品( 如:高 階 電 競、

因應 AI 伺服器龐大資金需求 ，董事會於 2026 年 8 月通過將發行上限達 15 億美元(約
新台幣 483 億元)的海外無擔保可轉換公司債(ECB)，並且計畫以研華(2395)持股發行
10~11 億美元的海外可交換公司債(EB)。

IFA 2026 展出搭載 NVIDIA RTX Spark 的 ProArt PC

NVIDIA AI 與 RTX 繪圖效能結合在單一晶片中(Blackwell RTX GPU+20 核心 Crace 
CPU)，這也是 NVIDIA 首次跨足 Windows PC 處理器市場市場。該晶片主要搭載於
輕薄 NB 與高效能迷你桌上型電腦，目標客戶為創作者、AI 開發者、電競玩家。

2481.TT 強茂 
 AI 不泡沫 功率就有夢

[調升評價]   
   公司簡介 
強茂為台灣功率半導體與分離式元件製造大廠，公司採垂直整合佈局，擁有 3 座前段
晶圓製造廠，及 5 座海內外後段封裝測試據點。 
投資評等/目標價 重點摘要 
買進 208 
【 前 次 投 資 建 議 】區 間 操 作，150 
【大盤指數/股價】45849 / 168.5

➢ 2Q26 獲利符合預期 
強茂 2Q26 營收 39.56 億元，QoQ+15.62%，YoY+16.34%，毛利率
33.22%， 稅後淨利4.88 億元 ，QoQ+69.58%，YoY+93.85%，EPS 1.28
元，獲利大致符合預期。毛利率維持季增趨勢，但營業費用比預期高，
主因是客戶拉貨強勁，備貨及物流相關費用多及提列獎金所致。 
➢ 下半年營收、獲利逐季向上 
AI 伺服器、車用及小訊號產品訂單能見度佳，強茂 B/B ratio 值維持在
1.3~1.5 高檔位置，3Q26 新增部份產能，加上產品調漲，預期營收及毛
利率持續向上；4Q26 有機會切入另一家 CSP 供應鏈，年底增加封測產
能(小訊號為主)、越南 TOREX 預計在 1Q27 交割，整體而言營運動能無
虞。預估強茂 3Q26 營收 42.51 億元，QoQ+7.47%，YoY+30.02%，
毛利率 34.44%，稅後淨利 5.58 億元，QoQ+14.39%，YoY+84.08%，
EPS 1.46 元。 
➢ 歐美重心轉移、非紅供應鏈，台 IDM 廠有訂價權 
在 AI 需求強勁推動下，單機櫃功耗持續上升並導入高壓直流電架構，帶
動功率元件量價同步提升，應用於伺服器功率元件年複合成長率遠高於
產業平均，在產能吃緊情況下，歐美 IDM 廠將重心轉向利潤較佳的高
壓、SiC 以及低壓大電流的 DrMOS 核心市場，排擠中低壓功率元件產
能。同時，安世、揚傑等中資背景企業面臨去中化，伺服器、車用工控
等領域加速將訂單轉向台廠。 
➢ 預估強茂 2027 年 EPS 6.95 元，建議買進，目標價 208 元 
投研部預估強茂 2026 年營收 159.39 億元，YoY+21.73%，稅後淨利
19.21 億元，YoY+61.22%，EPS 5.03 元；預估 2027 年營收 183.38 億
元，YoY+15.05%，稅後淨利 26.57 億元，YoY+38.29%，EPS 6.95 元。
目前強茂 PE 24X ，2026 年最高本益比已超越 2017~2018 年、
2020~2022 年功率元件景氣循環 20X~30X 區間，AI 伺服器的應用有
助拉升功率元件產業及公司評價，給予強茂買進建議，目標價 208 元
(2027F PE 30X)。

2481 強茂 加權指數

個股報告

公司簡介 
強茂股份有限公司成立於 1986 年，為台灣功率半導體與分離式元件製造大廠，公司
採垂直整合(IDM)佈局，擁有 3 座前段晶圓製造廠(4 吋、6 吋、8 吋各一座)，及 5 座
海內外後段封裝測試據點。 
1H26 產品營收比重 ： 金氧半場效電晶體(MOSFET)佔 32%， 蕭特基二極體(Schottky)
佔 23%，整流器(Rectifier)佔 12%，突波抑制器(TVS)佔 9%，靜電防護二極體(ESD 
Diode)佔 7%，齊納二極體(Zener)佔 6%，開關二極體(Switching)佔 6%，雙極性接
面電晶體(BJT)佔 4%，碳化矽(SiC)佔 1%。若以市場應用來看，車用電子佔營收比重
35%為最大，消費型電子佔 20%，電腦運算佔 16%，工控&綠能佔 13%，電源供應
用佔 12%，通訊佔 4%，其中 AI 人工智能應用佔 11%。 
圖 1：強茂產品組合

資料來源：強茂，國票投顧 
2Q26 獲利符合預期

淨利 4.88 億元，QoQ+69.58%，YoY+93.85%，EPS 1.28 元，獲利大致符合預期。
毛利率維持季增趨勢，但營業費用比預期高，主因是客戶拉貨強勁，備貨及物流相關
費用多及提列將金所致。

### Page 3

- 3 -

個股報告 
下半年營收、獲利逐季向上 
AI 伺服器、車用及小訊號產品訂單能見度佳，強茂 B/B ratio 值維持在 1.3~1.5 高檔
位置，強茂 8 月營收 13.76 億元，MoM-4.89%，YoY+29.82%，3Q26 新增部份產
能，加上產品調漲，預期營收及毛利率持續向上，4Q26 有機會切入另一家 CSP 供應
鏈，年底增加封測產能(小訊號為主)、越南 TOREX 預計在 1Q27 交割，整體而言營運
動能無虞。預估強茂 3Q26 營收 42.51 億元，QoQ+7.47%，YoY+30.02%，毛利率
34.44%，稅後淨利 5.58 億元，QoQ+14.39%，YoY+84.08%，EPS 1.46 元。 
歐美重心轉移、非紅供應鏈，台 IDM 廠有訂價權 
在 AI 需求強勁推動下 ， 單機櫃功耗持續上升並導入高壓直流電架構 ， 帶動功率元件量
價同步提升，應用於伺服器功率元件年複合成長率遠高於產業平均，在產能吃緊情況
下，歐美 IDM 廠將重心轉向利潤較佳的高壓、SiC 以及低壓大電流的 DrMOS 核心市
場，排擠中低壓功率元件產能。同時，安世、揚傑等中資背景企業面臨去中化，伺服
器、車用工控等領域加速將訂單轉向台廠。 
目前成熟製程與後段封裝產能極度吃緊，具備晶圓代工、晶片設計到封裝完整布局的 
強茂等 IDM 廠，憑藉產品線的廣度及垂直整合優勢，能確保供貨不中斷並免受外包
產能排擠 ， 更實質掌握了稀缺產能的訂價權 ， 在這一波由AI 架構升級與地緣政治雙軌
驅動的轉單潮中，將成為優先且最大受惠者。 
機櫃功率愈大，功率元件量價同步提升 
AI 應用佔強茂營收比重 11%，多數來自運算托盤(compute tray)上 Power MOSFET、
Schottky、TVS 或小訊號等功率產品，運算托盤上功率元件產值佔整機櫃超過一半，
主要核心在整合式供電模組(DrMOS)，DrMOS 技術門檻高，由歐美大廠主導，台廠
不易切入，但供電相數的增加，將同步提升對周邊功率元件的需求量。 
以 GB300 為例，單顆 GPU 功率 1.4kW，電壓 0.8V，核心負載輸出電流是 1,750A，
根據單顆高密度 DrMOS / SPS 晶片的最大承載電流(70A~130A)規格去設計供電相
數(Power Phase) ，每一相數都必須配置一套完整的硬體元件，當 GPU 功率提升至
2.3kW (Vera Rubin) ，GPU 功率增加 60%，供電相數及功率元件需求量亦將等幅放
大。 強茂功率元件已成功打入CSP 大廠 ， 未來將持續受惠運算托盤上功率元件量的提
升及質的改變(如 TVS 相對大電流，晶粒面積變大，單價提升)，產業成長趨勢明確。 
預估強茂 2027 年 EPS 6.95 元，建議買進，目標價 208 元 
投研部預估強茂 2026 年營收 159.39 億元，YoY+21.73%，稅後淨利 19.21 億元，
YoY+61.22%，EPS 5.03 元；預估 2027 年營收 183.38 億元，YoY+15.05%，稅後淨
利 26.57 億元，YoY+38.29%，EPS 6.95 元。目前強茂 PE 24X，2026 年最高本益比
已超越 2017~2018 年、2020~2022 年功率元件景氣循環 20X~30X 區間，AI 伺服
器的應用有助拉升功率元件產業及公司評價， 給予強茂買進 建議，目標價 208 元
(2027F PE 30X)。

## Document 068: 2548華固 第一金

### Page 1

楊凱云 
(886-2)2563-1666 
pineapple.yang@firstsec.com.tw

華固(2548) 
買進, TP 111 (潛在報酬 18.59%)  
買進 
Price(09/17):93.6 
Target Price：111

展望 2026年，完工案量與前一年相當，憑藉優異銷售策略與品牌
聲量，住宅案已全數完銷，商辦則持續與潛在買方洽談中，若期限
內未能找到整棟購置客戶，將改採分層銷售模式，預計年底前去化
約 50%，後續若順利完成整棟出售，則不排除上修獲利預估。展
望後市，公司持續獵地推案，目前建案能見度至 2034年，銷售中
總銷逾千億元，待推出總銷逾 900 億元，顯示公司中長期業績成
長無虞，惟短期受房市景氣及銷售速度放緩影響，業績表現仍存在
不確定性。股利方面，公司過往現金股利最低配發5元，且現金股
利配發率長期維持 70~80%，推估 2027 年現金股利有望達 6~7
元。綜合以上，研究部預估 2026、2027年 EPS 7.28、9.78元，
以 1.4*PBR評價，給予目標價 111元，投資建議為買進。 
 近期投資評等  營運概況 
1H26 營收 57.07億元，YoY+181.4%，稅後損益 9.43 億元，
YoY+307%，EPS 2.92 元，略低於研究部預期。上半年營收
主要來自新案「華固上文林」持續交屋挹注，全案總銷 61 億
元，已順利於8M26 完成過戶。獲利方面，1H26 毛利率 27%，
YoY-5.8ppt，由於新推案土地成本較高，加上興建期間物料及
工資上漲，導致利潤率略低於預期。

 公司展望 
展望 2026 年 ， 全年將迎來三大建案完工交屋 ， 合計總銷為179
億元，YoY-2.8%，分別為「華固一莊」(總銷 41 億元，完銷，
8M26 交屋)、「華固上文林」(總銷 61 億元，完銷，1H26交
屋)、「華固時代置地」(總銷 77 億元，持續與潛在客戶洽談
整棟出售，若期限內未能找到整棟購置客戶，公司將改採分層
銷售模式，預計年底前去化約 50%)。獲利方面，住宅案因土
地成本墊高，個案毛利率應介於25~26%之間；商辦產品則因
市場降溫，成交價格與利潤率回歸合理水準，毛利率預估約
28~30%，使全年合併毛利率將較 2025 年下滑，預估為
26.8%，YoY-1.43ppt。綜合以上，研究部預估 2026 年營收
140.85 億元， YoY-22.77%，稅後損益 23.27 億元，
Yo-28.21%，EPS 7.28元。

展望 2027 年，預計有三大建案完工交屋，合計總銷為205 億

 元，YoY+14.5%，分別為「華固頂匯」(總銷 108 億元，銷售
率 70~80%，預留60戶保留戶，將於完工後以成屋形式銷售，
預計 3Q27 完工交屋)、「華固豐匯」(總銷 47 元，銷售率
35~40%，4Q27 完工交屋)、「華固慕川」(總銷 50 億元，銷
售率 95~100%，4Q27 完工交屋)。由於台中地區銷售動能相
對疲弱，研究部認為「華固頂匯」及「華固豐匯」於完工前達
成完銷仍有一定難度，加上兩案完工時程接近年底，實際入帳
時程仍具不確定性，故預估整體入帳率約 60%，若加計「華
固時代置地」順利完銷，全年營收有望挑戰 160 億元大關。
綜合以上，研究部預估 2027 年營收 166.3 億元，
YoY+18.06%，稅後損益31.28 億元，YoY+34.43%，EPS 9.78
元。

 推案計畫及銷售中個案概況 
銷售中個案： 「華固頂匯」總銷108 億元，銷售率70~80%(僅
開放 200 戶銷售，剩餘 60 戶預計採成屋銷售)；「華固豐匯」
總銷 47 億元，銷售率 35~40%(出現少量退戶)；「華固慕川」
總銷 50 億元，銷售率95~100%； 「華固織幸」總銷33 億元，
銷售率 95~100%； 「吾雙」總銷67 億元，銷售率95~100%、
「華固芙心」 總銷53 億元，銷售率95~100%、 「華固四季匯」
總銷 54 億元，銷售率35~40%、「華固譽誠」總銷54 億元，
銷售率 85~90%、「華固創匯園區」總銷 325 億元，銷售率
25~30%。

2027年公司將再推出 「央北二案」(總銷64億元 ， 單價105~110
萬/坪，總價 4~5 千萬)、「松勇路案」(總銷 70億元，250 萬
/坪，2~3 億/戶，合計總銷為 134 億元，預計於 2030~2031
年完工交屋。

市況方面，2H24 起國內房市受限貸令及信用管制措施影響，
台中地區產品銷售速度趨緩，雙北地區則相對穩健，因此公司
未來獵地方向將回歸雙北，並以 60%住宅+40%廠辦為目標。
隨房市移轉量及房價已明顯降溫，因此央行近期放寬第二戶貸
款成數至 7 成，有助於提振換屋需求及購屋信心，最壞情況已
過，整體銷售動能有望回溫。

土地庫存方面，尚有逾 900 億元待推案將陸續於 2027~2030
年推出，並於 2034 年前完工挹注，顯示公司中長期業績成長

報告日期： 2026 / 09 / 10 研 究 員：林奕頎（ Yichi Lin ） 
壹、投 資 建 議 
一.預期  202 6 年 稅後 EPS + 8.22 元， YoY + 91%，創  18 年新高  
2024 年全年  BDI 平均  1,755  ； 2025 年全年  BDI 平均  1,681 。 
2026 年（截至 9 月 8 日）  BDI 平均  2,5 12： 
1Q / 2026 單季 BDI 平均  1,955； 2Q / 2026 單季 BDI 平均  2,751。 
3Q / 2026 單季（截至 9 月 8 日） BDI 平均  2,9 28。 
2026 年 9 月 8 日 單日  BDI 3,5 84。 運價 強勢，推升航商獲利 。 
二.美伊衝 突日益激烈 ，荷姆斯海峽封閉，油輪運價快速上漲  
裕民 船隊有  3 艘原油油輪  及 1 艘成品油輪 。  美伊 停戰 破局 ： 
原油油輪 運價指數：從 7 月 2 日 1,850 ，漲至 9 月 8 日 2,938 ，
累計漲幅  59%  ； 成品油輪 運價指數：從 7 月 7 日 995，漲至
9 月 8 日 1,702，累計漲幅  71%。 油輪運價 上漲，裕民將受惠。  
三.預期 4Q 是 2026 年單季獲利高峰 ， 稅後EPS + 2.41 元，YoY + 55%  
(1) 美伊衝突， 荷姆斯海峽 封閉 ，天然氣供給中斷 ； 4Q 取暖
旺季，各國 將被迫 以燃煤取代天然氣，推升海運燃煤需求 。 
(2) 聯合國預期 超級聖嬰現象至少將持續到 2027 年 2 月，極端
氣候橫行 ； 巴拿馬運河缺水 嚴峻，持續降低船舶通行量，
將壓抑海運供給 。  即使超級聖嬰解除；  依過去 5 年資料
（參閱圖 1）， 巴拿馬運河季節性乾季最低水位會落在 5 月。 
(3) 散裝航運以  4Q 為例行的季節性 旺季 。 
基於 (1) ~ (3) ，宏遠預期 4Q 將是裕民  2026 年單季獲利高峰。  
四.基於保守，估 2027 年 BDI 僅 2,490 ，裕民稅後 EPS + 7.10 元 
2026 年 9 月 8 日 單日  BDI 指數  3,5 84。 
宏遠 基於保守 原則，假設  三大利空在 2027 年落實 ： 
(1) 荷姆斯 海峽不再受阻 ，天然氣供給上升，壓抑煤炭需求 。 
(2) 超級 聖嬰解 除，巴拿馬運河不再缺水。  
(3) 全球散裝 船舶 下水量大增 。 
宏遠 估 2027 年 BDI 平均值僅  2,490； 裕民稅後 EPS + 7.10 元。 
若上述三項利空沒有落實，則必須調高裕民  2027 年獲利預估 。 
五.建議： 4Q 旺季  遇上  戰爭及聖嬰；  估 2026 年獲利，18 年新高  
2026 年截至 9 月 8 日，BDI 指數都 大幅 高於 2025 年同期（參閱
圖 2）； 此外，美伊戰爭造成 荷姆斯海峽中斷 ，須以燃煤取代
天然氣，推升散裝海運需求；  而 超級聖嬰 帶來全球乾旱，即
將撞上  巴拿馬運河  每年 12 月 ~ 隔年 5 月的 季節性 乾季（參
閱圖 1），巴拿馬運河缺水將更加嚴重；  宏遠 估裕民 2026 年稅
後 EPS  + 8.22 元，YoY + 91%，創 18 年新高 ； 持續 建議買進 。

貳、公 司 概 況 
一.公司簡介  
截至 2026 年 5 月（法說資料），裕民實際經營船隻為 71 艘：  
海岬型船   25 艘：自有 22 艘 + 3 艘合資。  
巴拿馬型船 24 艘：自有 14 艘 + 7 艘代管  + 3 艘合資 。 
輕便型      8 艘（自有） 。 
水泥船      4 艘（自有） 。 
油輪        4 艘：透過  環能海運 （持股 40% ）。 
CTV 離岸風電人員運輸船 6 艘（自有  ； 裕民風能航運 ）。

在建船隻  12 艘，交船時程 ： 
202 6 年： 輕便型  1 艘（預期 11 月交付）。 
202 7 年 及 2028 年： 
海岬型  4 艘 + 輕便型  5 艘 + 風電工程船  2 艘。

二.營運概況 及展望  
1.超級聖嬰 造成缺水  撞上  巴拿馬運河乾季，壓抑海運供給  
2026 年 5 月，全球進入聖嬰年，加劇全球乾旱與極端高溫 的
氣候異常 。 聯合國  世界氣象組織（ WMO ）表示， 這波聖嬰
現象，可能是過去  40 年最強一次，幾乎肯定會持續到  2027
年 2 月，很可能打破  2023 年 ~ 2024 年的紀錄 。

巴拿馬運河溝通大西洋與太平洋，節省海運船舶至少  3,000
海哩的航程，航行時間縮短  2 週左右。

但由於 大西洋與太平洋的海平面和水流高度不同，船隻 要透
過 巴 拿 馬 運 河 ，橫 跨 美 洲 ， 必 須依 靠 巴 拿 馬 運 河 的船閘 注
水，採取類似 爬樓梯一樣 的升降 動作 ，讓船 隻從海平面被提
升到高處的加通湖，穿過湖面後，再降回另一側的海平面。
每艘船舶 每次通過船閘，都需要消耗  加通湖  數以百萬加侖
計算的淡水。

因此， 加通湖的水量多寡 ，會影響巴拿馬運河的運作效率與
容量 ，一旦缺水，巴拿馬運河每日通行船舶數量就會減少。

觀察過去 5 年（2021 年 ~ 2025 年）加通湖的水位資料（參閱
圖 1） ，通常會從每年 5 月，逐月爬升到  12 月；  但 2026 年
超級聖嬰的乾旱，造成目前 9 月水位不升反降 。 
以現貨為主，長約為輔 
總船隊 71 艘 
全球 超級聖嬰 極端氣候 
從 2026 年 5 月 至少持續 
至 2027 年 2 月

巴拿美運河 季節性乾季 從 
每年 12 月 持續至 隔年 5 月

聖嬰 + 乾季 
預期巴拿馬運河 要解除缺水 
最快必須等到 2027 年 2H

2026 年 5 月起，全球 超級聖嬰 ，帶來的 乾旱，至少將持續到
2027 年 2 月 ； 如此，將撞上  加通湖  每年  例行 的 季節 性 
乾季 （每年 12 月 ~ 隔年 5 月 ； 參閱圖 1 ）。

因此， 巴拿馬運河（ 加通湖）的缺水很可能會持續加重，最
快的解除時間可能要等到  2027 年 2H。

巴拿馬運河  每日正常通行量  36 ~ 40 艘。 
2023 年 ~ 2024 年 全球 聖嬰 造成乾旱  巴拿馬運河的 每日船
舶通行量最低降至  18 艘。

目前  加通湖  尚未進入  季節性乾季，但 受制於全球聖嬰現
象， 加通湖  水位  已經 提前 明顯降低 ； 巴拿馬運河管理當
局， 已經 宣布，巴拿馬運河每日船舶通行數量， 2026 年 9 月
3 日降至  34 艘 ； 9 月 15 日降至  32 艘。

宏遠預期， 巴拿馬運河降低每日船舶通行數量的趨勢，將日
益嚴重， 壓抑所有的海運供給 ，將有利於推升各型船舶的運
價，拉高海運航商獲利 。

圖 1：加通湖 過去 5 年 每月平均水位變化 ； 5 月 走升至 12 月（雨季） ； 12 月 再走跌至 5 月（乾季） 
超級聖嬰帶來乾旱，造成 2026 年 9 月水位 不升反降 。

單位：英尺。 
資料來源： 網路公開資訊  ； 宏遠  。

2.散裝 BDI 走勢，隱示  2026 年獲利  將大幅優於  2025 年 
2026 年截至 9 月 8 日，散裝 BDI 運價走勢都優於 2025 年同期
表現（ 參閱圖 2） 。 
受惠於 BDI 的強勁走勢 ，裕民 ： 
1Q / 2026 稅後 EPS + 1.17 元，優於  1Q / 2025 同期  + 0.27 元。 
2Q / 2026 稅後 EPS + 2.34 元，優於  2Q / 2025 同期  + 0.77 元。

從 BDI 的表現來看：  
1Q / 2026 （過年淡季） BDI 平均  1,955 。 
2Q / 2026 （美伊戰爭爆發， 燃煤替代天然氣） BDI 平均  2,751 。 
3Q / 2026 （截至 9 月 8 日） BDI 平均  2,928。 
2026 年 9 月 8 日 單日  BDI  3,584 。

4Q 是散裝 航運的 季節性 旺季，遇上， 荷姆斯海峽封閉  推升  
煤 炭 的 海 運 需 求  及   巴 拿 馬 運 河 缺 水 降低  海運船舶供
給（繞道等同減少供給） ； 預期 4Q 的 BDI 將會持續強勁。

BDI 漲勢 強勁，宏遠預期 裕民  3Q ~ 4Q / 2026 單季獲利 YoY 大
幅成長 ； 未來， 3Q 及 4Q 財報發布 ，將激勵股價表現 。

Yageo (2327.TW)

3
Yageo
Valuation
We set our target price at NT$1,450, based on 35x average 2027E-28E earnings. We value Yageo based on PE as we believe the
company’s earnings performance and industry dynamics would be the key focuses of the market. Our target multiple of 35x is
at the higher end of the trading range since 2022. During this period the company has gone through the cycles, successfully
integrated the acquired business to enhance its product offerings, and started to deliver stable margins and earnings.

Risks
Key downside risks to our target price and rating include: 1) slower-than-expected demand recovery; 2) intensified peer and
pricing competition; 3) execution risks associated with M&A integration and synergies; and 4) FX and interest rate fluctuations.
Prepared for Kevin Lu

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

our ratings distribution table for our Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside rating system. However, we reiterate that we do
not consider No View to be a recommendation. For all Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside calls, risk exists that the
catalyst(s) and associated share-price movement will not materialize as expected.

## Document 083: 260909_daiwa_ardentec-buy-initiation

Source PDF: all report pdf/260909_daiwa_ardentec-buy-initiation.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-09-09
Pages: 24
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Taiwan Information Technology

Investment case: We initiate coverage of Ardentec, a Taiwanese pure 
testing provider serving foundries, IDMs and fabless chipmakers, with a 
Buy (1) rating. We believe Ardentec is transitioning from an auto/industrial 
test vendor into an AI/HPC wafer-sort powerhouse, supported by a 35% 
revenue CAGR (2025-28E) and gross margin expansion to 45% by 2028E.

Longtan fab ramp with premium hourly rate. We expect the 7-story 
Longtan mega-fab, which is developing at one floor per quarter to deliver 
significant revenue contribution (c.TWD450m+/quarter per floor). We 
believe this underpins a structural upgrade in revenue, as advanced wafer 
sorting for application-specific integrated circuits (ASIC) commands an 
hourly billing rate nearly double that of legacy testing. Furthermore, we 
believe strategic collaborations with key Taiwan foundry leaders position 
Ardentec to capture expanding AI/HPC test allocations moving forward.

Gross margin increase driven by high utilisation rate. As its key peers 
prioritise merchant AI GPUs, displaced mobile/RF/networking orders are 
refilling Ardentec’s fully depreciated tester lines, lifting mature utilisation. 
Test-time inflation (2-3x on complex ASICs) anchors Longtan capacity at a 
90%+ utilisation rate. As fixed costs comprise 40-45% of COGS, this drives 
blended gross margin from 36% in 2025 to 45% in 2028E, with steady 
operating expense (9-10% of sales) flowing into operating profit.

Turnkey E/O testing for CPO and non-dilutive expansion. Leveraging 
synergies with Giga-Solution, Raytek (7873 TT; 25% stake), and strategic 
partner Fabrinet (FN US, NR), Ardentec builds a comprehensive electro-
optical test flow aligned with TSMC’s compact universal photonic engine 
(COUPE) platform. While co-packaged optics (CPO) testing accounts for 
<5% of near-term sales, it secures an early-mover moat in optical I/O.

Catalysts: 1) Completion of 7-floor capacity with premium rates and higher 
UTR; 2) peer spillover orders refilling fully depreciated capacity, expanding 
gross margin; 3) higher-than-expected ASIC and CPO contribution.

Valuation: Driven by a structural earnings inflection fuelled by Longtan fab 
expansion, expanding AI ASIC test pricing premiums, and high-margin peer 
spillover orders, we initiate Ardentec with a Buy (1) rating and 12-month TP 
of TWD340, based on a target PER of 25x applied to our 4-quarter-forward 
EPS, at the top end of its historical 10-25x range but supported by a 48% 
2025-28E EPS CAGR and peer AI test valuations of 18-24x.

Risks: 1) Delays in capacity expansion; 2) pricing pressure as a second 
supplier; and 3) slow automotive and industrial recovery.

### Page 3

Ardentec (3264 TT): 9 September 2026

How do we justify our view? 
Growth outlook Valuation Earnings revisions

Growth outlook

Ardentec: revenue and gross margin outlook   
We forecast Ardentec to enter a structural multi-year 
revenue growth cycle, rising from TWD14.0bn in 2025 to 
TWD34.4bn by 2028E, implying a 34.8% CAGR. This 
revenue growth is supported by the phased ramp-up of its 
Longtan fab facility, increasing exposure to premium AI 
ASIC and HPC wafer testing, and broad-based ASP 
expansion driven by tighter mature-node test capacity. 
Meanwhile, we expect improving utilisation coupled with 
growing contributions from fully depreciated legacy testers, 
to drive gross margin expansion from 36.3% in 2025 to 
45.0% by 2028E.

Source: Company, Daiwa forecasts

Valuation   Ardentec: 1-year-forward PER bands    
Given the current structural mix transition from cyclical 
automotives towards high-margin AI/HPC custom ASICs, 
amplified gross margin and operating leverage from the 
Longtan ramp, and ROE inflection likely to surpass its 2022 
historical peak of c.23% over 2026-28E, we initiate 
coverage of Ardentec with a Buy (1) rating and 12-month 
TP of TWD340, based on a target PER of 25.0x applied to 
our 4-quarter-forward EPS, at the top end of its historical 
10-25x range but supported by a 48% 2025-28E EPS 
CAGR and peer AI test valuations of 18-24x.

Source: Bloomberg

Earnings revisions

Ardentec: Bloomberg-consensus earnings revisions    
Bloomberg consensus raised its 2026-27E EPS estimates 
by 30-45%, driven by the rapid Longtan Floor 1 ramp, 
premium ASIC hourly rates, and high-margin peer spillover 
orders. The upward revisions likely reflect the completion 
of the remaining floors, test-time inflation driving higher 
testing hours, and internal capacity tightness unlocking 
pricing power across mature lines. Ardentec is currently 
covered by 4 brokers. We believe the market is overlooking 
this stock despite its structural fundamental turnaround 
outlook and depressed valuation.

Source: Bloomberg

Company profile 
Kinsus was founded in 2000 as a subsidiary of Asustek. The company mainly focus on niche ball grid 
array (BGA) substrates to avoid price competition among mainstream products like graphics card and 
chipsets, and it has become top-3 flip chip manufacturer in global. Besides, its manufacturing sites are 
based in Taiwan and China.

### Page 6

Ardentec (3264 TT): 9 September 2026 
From Automotive to AI/HPC growth engines 
A premier pure play in wafer testing 
Founded in October 1999 and headquartered in Hukou, Hsinchu, Ardentec Corporation is 
a premier semiconductor testing pure play, ranking among the top three pure test service 
providers in Taiwan in terms of revenue. While semiconductor testing is inherently a 
capital- and technology-intensive arena dominated by foundry and integrated OSAT titans 
such as TSMC (2330 TT, TWD2,465, Buy [1]; covered by Daiwa analyst Rick Hsu) and 
ASE Group (3711 TT, TWD640, Outperform [2]; covered by Daiwa analyst Rick Hsu), 
Ardentec establishes a resilient, differentiated market presence through two structural 
pillars:

• Strategic dual-sourcing anchor and supply chain balancer. As advanced packaging 
and testing capacity is increasingly centralised within leading foundries and mega-
OSATs, tier-1 fabless leaders actively de-risk operations by qualifying secondary 
sources. Ardentec functions as an independent, pure-play testing alternative, providing 
foundry-aligned engineering standards alongside commercial flexibility. This positioning 
allows tier-1 chipmakers to diversify vendor concentration, secure dedicated capacity 
allocations during cyclical tight supply, and mitigate single-point manufacturing 
bottlenecks.

• Technical versatility supported by automotive qualification standards. Ardentec 
maintains a high revenue exposure to automotive and industrial IC testing – sectors that 
demand zero-defect reliability, ultra-wide temperature ranges, and complex mixed-signal 
test protocols. This operational baseline provides significant cross-application versatility. 
During automotive cycle downturns, qualified test cells and probe architectures can be 
reconfigured to support compute, edge AI, and high-frequency communication ICs, 
helping dampen gross margin volatility and stabilising baseline fleet utilisation across 
broader semiconductor cycles.

Ardentec: revenue by product    Ardentec: revenue by application

Source: Company, Daiwa forecasts  Source: Company

Longtan: seven-floor mega-fab to scale AI 
Spanning seven vertical cleanroom floors, the company’s Longtan facility began a multi-
year construction timeline starting in 2026, manufacturing semiconductor chips for tier-1 
foundry customers. Management confirmed Floor 1 achieved commercial mass production 
in 3Q26; its +15% MoM revenue inflection in July validated the expansion. At peak 
utilisation, we estimate each fully loaded floor will generate c.TWD150m per month (TWD 
400-500m quarterly) in revenue. With tooling and equipment installations tracking a 
predictable one-floor-per-quarter cadence, each incremental floor can accommodate a 
maximum of 100 high-end automated test equipment (ATE) platforms.

Commanding an hourly billing rate nearly double that of legacy CP testing (95-100% 
premium over legacy CP testing), we estimate each newly commissioned floor will drive 
the company’s top line by 8-10%. We believe this rapid deployment will transform AI/HPC 
sorting from an ancillary segment into Ardentec’s primary growth engine and top-line 
contributor over our forecast horizon.

While Floor 1 capex was self-funded to fast-track qualification, subsequent floor 
expansions are transitioning toward customer-consigned tester models, which will reduce 
balance sheet risk and secure committed wafer allocation. We believe the fleet is custom-
tailored for leading US hyperscaler ASIC roadmaps. By locking in application-specific test 
setups and eliminating idle downtime associated with frequent load-board changeovers, 
Ardentec is able to achieve industry-leading throughput, uncompromised test cell 
efficiency, and maximised asset turns, per management.

Ardentec: capacity expansion    Ardentec: revenue contribution from its Longtan Fab

Source: Company, Daiwa forecasts  Source: Company, Daiwa forecasts

Proprietary accelerator roadmaps across cloud service providers (CSP) – including AWS 
Trainium/Inferentia, Meta MTIA, and Microsoft Maia – feature starkly heterogeneous probe 
card architectures, thermal docking requirements, and test firmware protocols. We believe 
the modular, multi-story footprint at the company’s Longtan facility provides the physical 
isolation necessary to construct dedicated, IP-secure production cleanrooms for multiple 
hyperscaler customers. As custom AI ASIC testing accounts for a rapidly expanding share 
of the company’s revenue mix relative to legacy automotive exposure, we believe this 
structural diversification serves as a strong catalyst for a rerating for Ardentec towards tier-
1 compute OSAT multiples.

Broad-based UTR inflection on peer capacity spillover 
While automotive and consumer segments navigate cyclical normalisation, Ardentec’s non-
AI capacity is staging a powerful recovery, per management. Based on our channel 
checks, its key OSAT peers (eg, KYEC: 2449 TT, not rated) have aggressively reallocated 
leading-edge test fleets toward mega-cap merchant AI GPUs, creating severe capacity 
bottlenecks for mobile application processor (AP), radio frequency (RF), and networking IC 
designers (such as MediaTek – 2454 TT, TWD4,625, Buy [1], covered by Rick Hsu). 
Therefore, Ardentec is capturing these high-volume, displaced wafer sorting (or chip 
probing, CP) and final test (FT) allocations. This order influx swiftly rebounds mature and 
communication line utilisation rates from trough levels (below 65%) back toward the 80%+ 
threshold, turning legacy capacity into a high-cash-generation baseline with zero asset 
drag.

Semiconductor testing carries a high fixed-cost structure heavily weighted toward 
equipment depreciation and engineering overhead. Historically, once aggregate corporate 
UTR crosses the critical 65% fixed-cost absorption threshold, incremental revenue drops 
directly to the bottom line with minimal variable costs (primarily power and consumables).

Advanced-node ASICs feature extreme sensitivity to thermal dissipation and signal

Next-gen CPO and silicon photonics testing 
Beyond the immediate custom ASIC cycle, Ardentec is securing its next structural growth 
frontier through advanced co-packaged optics (CPO) and silicon photonics (SiPh) wafer-
level testing. As hyperscale AI clusters push copper interconnects to physical transmission 
limits, the industry is transitioning towards optical I/O, which requires testing to shift from 
purely electrical verification to complex electro-optical (E/O) wafer sorting (CP). Unlike 
standard digital logic testing, optical probing demands sub-micron automated fibre 
alignment, precise laser wavelength calibration, optical power loss measurement, and 
specialised thermal-stability testing across photonic integrated circuits (PIC) and electronic 
integrated circuits (EIC) before advanced heterogeneous integration.

transceiver verification. Concurrently, Raytek bolsters the group's specialised optical probe 
station capabilities and precision electro-optical test fixtures.

Ardentec (3264 TT): 9 September 2026 
Market Analysis 
Structural shift in the IC testing industry 
Reiterating the key message in our Taiwan Testing Interface Initiation: paradigm shift 
accelerated by AI report, we believe the semiconductor testing paradigm is shifting. In the 
multi-die and 2.5D/3D packaging era (eg, TSMC CoWoS, SoIC), wafer testing (chip 
probing, CP) is no longer a commoditised, post-fab defect screening step – it has evolved 
into a mission-critical gating process. We summarise the critical paradigm shift as follows:

• The known good die (KGD) imperative. Heterogeneous integration bundles high-
value compute dies with multiple high bandwidth memory (HBM) stacks. Packaging an 
untested or marginally defective die risks scrapping the entire multi-thousand-dollar 
subsystem. This zero-tolerance threshold elevates CP from sample-based checks to 
exhaustive, full-spec validation across extreme thermal ranges.

• Structural Test-Time Inflation (2-3x). High reticle-limit AI/HPC custom silicon, 
expanding power envelopes (>700W), and thousands of high-speed interconnect 
channels have doubled or tripled test times per wafer. Consequently, automated test 
equipment (ATE) fleet demand is decoupling from raw silicon unit growth, driven instead 
by compounding tester hours.

• Next-Gen CPO Frontier. Rapid silicon tape-out cadences demand high-density vertical 
probe cards (VPC) and micro-cantilever interfaces supporting parallel multi-site testing. 
Beyond electrical testing, the emergence of CPO and SiPh requires active sub-micron 
optical alignment and electro-optical (E/O) sorting, creating multi-year technical barriers 
and pricing premiums for early movers.

Market dynamics: testing outperforms packaging 
According to Gartner (Semiconductor Assembly and Test Services, Worldwide, Sep 2025, 
Semiconductors and Electronics Team), total package and test market size is projected to 
increase from USD47.3bn to USD65.2bn over 2025-29E, representing an 8.4% CAGR 
over the period. Given that third-party market data typically blends packaging and testing 
into total OSAT, we granularly disaggregate historical revenue across major US and Taiwan 
vendors.

While consumer electronics endured a prolonged post-pandemic inventory correction over 
2022-25, the emergence of AI/HPC workloads created a pronounced divergence across 
the backend: historical industry data indicates that the testing sub-segment delivered 
materially higher year-over-year revenue growth than packaging over 2023-25, propelled 
by multi-die KGD mandates and structural test-time inflation.

Advantage of pure-play testing 
We think three reasons distinguish dedicated testing pure-plays from traditional integrated 
OSATs:

• Asset-light model and lean working capital mechanics. Integrated OSATs face 
substantial working capital exposure to expensive ABF substrates, precious metal 
bonding wires, and carrier materials. In contrast, pure-play testing carries virtually zero 
direct material inventory, insulating balance sheets from write-down risks. Furthermore, 
automated test equipment depreciation is concentrated directly within COGS, with 
capital intensity effectively moderated by customer-consigned tester allocations.

Global OSAT: Capex intensity

Source: Bloomberg

• Fleet flexibility and potential spillover orders. Unlike specialised packaging lines that 
require extensive physical retooling, standardised high-end ATE platforms exhibit 
exceptional fleet flexibility. Once test programs and interface hardware are qualified, 
testers can rapidly transition across diverse application domains – from premium mobile 
APs and automotive MCUs to complex custom ASICs – by swapping application-
specific load boards and high-density probe cards. As tier-1 OSATs concentrate their 
tester fleets on merchant AI GPU allocations, mobile AP, 5G RF, and high-speed 
networking chipmakers face severe capacity displacement. We believe pure-play test 
houses could benefit from a dual growth driver: rising demand for high-margin AI/HPC 
programs and incremental mobile and networking orders redirected to underutilised 
legacy tester capacity. This dynamic could decisively shift commercial bargaining 
leverage to pure testers, expanding hourly billing rates and unlocking structural gross 
margin leverage.

• IP neutrality and ecosystem balancing. North American hyperscalers designing 
proprietary custom silicon actively avoid monolithic vendor lock-in and potential IP 
conflicts within integrated supply chains. Pure-play testers provide a strictly neutral 
counterweight with uncompromised test cell isolation. Multi-story modular facilities 
deliver the physical segregation necessary to enforce proprietary security protocols and 
run dedicated engineering cells, allowing pure-play test houses to function as 
indispensable, IP-secure manufacturing partners across the advanced foundry 
ecosystem.

Financial analysis 
Revenue growth in 2026-28E 
We forecast Ardentec’s top-line to enter a multi-year expansion cycle, accelerating from 
TWD14.0bn in 2025 to TWD19.5bn in 2026E (+39.0% YoY), TWD26.8bn in 2027E 
(+37.5% YoY), and reaching TWD34.4bn in 2028E (+28.2% YoY). This represents a 3-year 
revenue CAGR of +34.8% (2025-28E), driven by a powerful confluence of volume 
expansion and structural pricing power across both advanced and mature test fleets.

First, we believe the orderly floor-by-floor commissioning of the 7-story Longtan facility 
delivers a predictable volume baseline, with each fully loaded cleanroom floor contributing 
c.TWD150m per month (c.TWD450m+ quarterly) in incremental run-rate revenue. Beyond 
pure wafer throughput, we expect revenue quality to see a significant mix upgrade, as 
advanced wafer sorting for hyperscaler AI ASICs and complex HPC processors command 
an hourly billing rate of nearly double (95-100% premium) that of traditional automotive and 
industrial IC testing, fundamentally elevating revenue density per tester hour.

Furthermore, as Ardentec prioritises engineering bandwidth, cleanroom footprint, and 
tester allocations toward high-margin advanced CP, legacy and mature-node test capacity 
faces internal supply tightness. This self-induced capacity crowding shifts pricing leverage 
back to Ardentec, allowing the company to enact upward price adjustments across lagging-
node test services and accelerating a broad-based, corporate-wide blended ASP 
expansion.

Ardentec (3264 TT): 9 September 2026 
Gross margin inflection driven by UTR surge  
We believe capacity utilisation rate serves as the single most critical determinant of 
Ardentec’s gross margin trajectory. Over the coming quarters, we project a structural 
rebound in corporate-wide utilisation driven by 2 powerful operational forces.

First, the rapid ramp of the Longtan advanced testing fab—operating at an optimal 85-90% 
UTR ceiling—will represent a rapidly expanding proportion of total capacity, immediately 
enriching the blended margin profile. Second, incoming spillover orders from tier-1 
communications and mobile AP clients are directly refilling legacy and mature testing lines. 
Because these mature tools are almost or already fully depreciated, incremental spillover 
revenue incurs virtually zero depreciation burden, generating exceptionally high operating 
leverage and outsized gross profit contribution, in our view.

Therefore, we estimate that this dual dynamic of high-end capacity scaling and fully 
amortised tool revitalisation will propel gross margins from 36.3% in 2025 to 42.3% in 
2026E, 44.5% in 2027E and reach 45.0% in 2028E.

Ardentec: gross margin

Source: Company, Daiwa forecasts

Earnings led by gross margin amid subdued operating 
leverage 
We believe that Ardentec’s earnings inflection is predominantly a story of gross margin and 
operating leverage rather than structural opex dilution. The high-fixed-cost nature of the 
wafer testing business—governed by multi-billion-dollar ATE depreciation and cleanroom 
overhead—is heavily concentrated within Cost of Goods Sold (COGS), per management. 
Consequently, the powerful fixed-cost absorption resulting from Longtan’s ramp and peer 
spillover orders directly fuels gross margin expansion, while operating leverage at the 
SG&A/opex level remains comparatively subdued.

Because high-end ASIC and electro-optical testing require tailored test program 
development, application-specific load board debugging and dedicated test engineering 
headcount for each CSP roadmap, we believe R&D and engineering talent function as 
quasi-variable expenses that scale in tandem with incremental floor additions. As a result, 
we project Ardentec’s opex-to-sales ratio to remain relatively stable in the 9-10% corridor 
over 2026-28E.

Balance sheet and cash flow 
While management has not provided official guidance due to dynamic cleanroom 
construction milestones and equipment lead-time variations, we model an orderly 
deployment cadence of one cleanroom floor per quarter at the Longtan mega-facility. 
Under this baseline, we forecast total capital expenditures (capex) to step up aggressively 
to TWD14.5bn in 2026E and TWD18.5bn in 2027E to support facility buildout and early tool 
installations.

To fund this expansion phase, Ardentec intends to prioritise low-cost bank borrowings over 
issuing overseas or domestic convertible bonds (ECB/CB), deliberately avoiding equity 
dilution to maximise EPS accretion during the upcoming earnings supercycle. 
Concurrently, management plans to optimise capital allocation by pragmatically calibrating 
its historical cash dividend pay-out ratio. We estimate the pay-out ratio will moderate 
slightly from 70% to 50% to retain internal cash for high-return AI capacity. Meanwhile, we 
believe the company actively negotiates customer-consigned tester models for subsequent 
cleanroom floors. This is a framework that insulates the cost structure from severe, front-
loaded depreciation burdens and channels capital directly into superior return on equity 
(ROE) growth.

Benefitting from disciplined financing, high asset turnover on advanced wafer sort lines and 
gross margin expansion, we project Ardentec's ROE to stage a structural recovery from the 
cyclical trough experienced during the 2023 automotive downturn, steadily increasing from 
15.1% to 32.1% over 2025-2027E.

Valuation and recommendation 
Initiating coverage with a Buy (1) rating 
Given the current structural mix transition from cyclical automotives towards high-margin 
AI/HPC custom ASICs, amplified gross margin and operating leverage from the Longtan 
ramp, and ROE inflection likely to surpass its 2022 historical peak of c.23% over 2026-
28E, we initiate coverage of Ardentec with a Buy (1) rating and 12-month TP of TWD340, 
based on a target PER of 25.0x applied to our 4-quarter-forward EPS. Our target multiple 
of 25.0x is supported by a 48% EPS CAGR over 2025-28E (PEG of c.0.52x).

Historically, Ardentec traded within a compressed band of 10-25x forward PER due to its 
cyclical automotive and industrial exposure. While the stock has begun to re-rate alongside 
recent EPS upgrades, we view this multiple expansion as far from complete. In addition, 
we believe Ardentec still trades at a noticeable discount (14.9x PER on 2027E EPS) vs. 
leading AI testing peers (KYEC trading at 18.7x PER, while ASE at 26.4x), leaving ample 
room for multiple expansion as its pure-play test economics and earnings growth outpace 
the broader backend sector.

Ardentec (3264 TT): 9 September 2026 
Risks to our call 
1. Delays in capacity expansion (primary risk). Delays in Longtan cleanroom and 
equipment installations, or customer qualifications could disrupt the floor-by-floor ramp, 
deferring high-margin hyperscaler ASIC revenue and diluting near-term gross margin 
leverage.  
2. Pricing pressure as a second-source supplier (secondary risk). Serving as a 
second-source supplier forces Ardentec to accept lower hourly rates than primary 
competitors. Furthermore, non-AI clients demand annual price cuts, limiting gross 
margin recovery unless capacity utilisation remains exceptionally high.  
3. Slow automotive and industrial recovery (secondary risk). Prolonged inventory 
adjustments in automotive and industrial markets continue to drag down IDM revenue 
share. Clients are placing short-term urgent orders rather than rebuilding long-term 
inventory, creating demand uncertainty for mature testing capacity.

### Page 18

18

Ardentec (3264 TT): 9 September 2026 
Appendix 
Company introduction 
Founded in 1999 and headquartered in Hsinchu, Ardentec is an independent 
semiconductor testing service provider. The company specialises in wafer testing (wafer 
sort) and final testing for memory, logic and mixed-signal integrated circuits. Its diverse 
portfolio addresses several sectors, including automotive electronics, 5G communications, 
storage and advanced AI/HPC ASICs.

Through strategic investments in Giga Solution and Raytek, Ardentec has also successfully 
expanded into RF testing and bumping capabilities. Its global customer base spans world-
class foundries, IDMs and fabless designers, such as MediaTek, Marvell, Texas 
Instruments, Infineon and NXP.

Ardentec’s core competitive advantage lies in its early leadership in automotive 
semiconductor testing, underpinned by ISO 26262 certification and stringent reliability 
standards that support strong customer stickiness and order stability. As an independent, 
pure-play testing provider, Ardentec serves as a neutral strategic partner to leading fabless 
companies, offering foundry-grade engineering capabilities, dedicated capacity and greater 
commercial flexibility.

Its role extends beyond that of a conventional second-source supplier: by providing an 
independent alternative to increasingly consolidated foundry and OSAT ecosystems, 
Ardentec helps customers diversify supply-chain risk, secure critical testing capacity and 
avoid potential bottlenecks. Backed by its state-of-the-art Longtan facility dedicated to 
advanced AI ASIC testing and a global manufacturing footprint across Taiwan, Nanjing, 
South Korea and Singapore, Ardentec is well positioned as an indispensable testing 
partner for tier-1 and emerging semiconductor customers.

Shareholder structure 
Ardentec’s shareholder base is well diversified, with no single controlling shareholder. 
Dresdner Bank AG is the largest holder with an 8.36% stake, followed by Macronix 
International (7.33%) and Vanguard Group (4.66%). The register also includes long-term 
institutional investors such as BlackRock, Dimensional Fund Advisors, BNP Paribas and 
Taiwan’s pension-related funds, providing a stable ownership structure and supporting 
governance transparency.

Summary: Driven by strong AI/HPC demand, all three companies set new revenue 
milestones: Winway (TWD 1.71bn, +6.6% MoM, +233.4% YoY), MPI (TWD 2.05bn, 
+13.4% MoM, +69.9% YoY), and CHPT (TWD 638m, +3.9% MoM, +54.3% YoY). With 
Winway’s capacity expansions tracking ahead of schedule, MPI set to begin shipping CPO 
insertion 3 products in 4Q26 alongside ASP hikes in late-3Q26, and CHPT nearing a 
qualification for its turnkey CPU ASIC project, the sector offers resilient sequential growth 
visibility through 2H26 and potential earnings upside heading into 2027.

What’s new: Following the conclusion of SEMICON Taiwan, we have observed a marked 
uptick in investor enquiries regarding the semiconductor test interface supply chain. As 
AI/HPC architectural transitions, and the increasing complexity of 2.5D/3D packaging drive 
structural content gains, investors have focused increasingly on capacity visibility and 
technological roadmaps across the socket and probe card segments. In the note, we recap 
the latest August monthly revenue figures and synthesize key market dynamics across our 
coverage universe.

WinWay Technology (6515 TT, TWD7,280, Buy [1]) 
• Aug revenue result: Monthly revenue reached TWD 1.71bn ( +6.6% MoM, +233.4% 
YoY), bringing 1-8M26 cumulative revenue to TWD 9.81bn (+97.9% YoY). Jul-Aug sales 
reached 72% of our 3Q26E revenue, tracking ahead of our expectations.

• Product mix & catalysts: Growth remains anchored by aggressive tape-outs from 
North American GPU leaders and cloud hyperscalers. Shipments of proprietary coaxial 
test sockets and advanced wafer-level vertical probe cards expanded sharply. While the 
market may expect meaningful revenue contributions from CPO sockets or probe cards, 
we believe it will account for mid-single digit in 2027E and high-single digit in 2028E.

• Outlook: The company maintained a constructive stance on order visibility and 
persistent demand-supply tightness. Management reaffirmed that 2Q26 marked the 
near-term cyclical trough, guiding for sequential revenue expansion on a QoQ basis 
moving forward. Our supply chain checks indicate that capacity expansion execution is 
tracking ahead of schedule compared with last month. With Winway poised to bring 
additional capacity online in 2027, we suggest investors monitor the company’s 2H26 
earnings call (mid-Nov) for further clarity on its 2027 capex and capacity roadmap.

• Valuation: We have a Buy (1) rating with a 12-month TP of TWD10,000, based on a 
target PER of 70x applied to our 4-quarter-forward EPS forecast. Based on our 
2027/28E EPS, the stock is currently trading at PERs of 36.9x/19.5x, vs. its previous 
highs of 80-90x. Downside risks: heavy reliance on major US AI chipmakers and; 2) 
intensifying competition that could erode design wins.

MPI Corporation (6223 TT, TWD5,615, Buy[1]) 
• Aug revenue result: Monthly revenue surpassed the TWD 2bn milestone for the first 
time, reaching TWD 2.05bn (+13.4% MoM, +69.9% YoY), bringing 1-8M26 cumulative 
revenue above TWD 13.0bn. Jul-Aug sales reached 63% of our 3Q26E revenue 
estimate, tracking slightly below our expectations.

• Product mix & catalysts: Vertical Probe Cards (VPC) and MEMS probe cards are

scheduled for delivery in 4Q26, setting the stage for mass production orders in 1H27.

• Outlook: Although management’s muted 3Q26 guidance initially fuelled investor 
scepticism, and the cumulative 2M26 run-rate is tracking slightly behind our quarterly 
forecast, we view the market’s pessimism as overly conservative. Strong late-quarter 
shipment seasonality, coupled with concentrated September revenue recognition for 
high-end VPC/MEMS probe cards under the newly effective pricing schedule should 
help bridge the gap and keep 3Q26 on track to meet or exceed our top-line 
expectations. Looking into 4Q26 and early 2027, the commercial rollout of CPO 
insertion 3 equipment and continued certification progress for insertion 2 provide a multi-
year growth runaway and durable operating leverage.

• Valuation: We have a Buy (1) rating with a 12-month TP of TWD8,415, based on a

2027/28E EPS, the stock is currently trading at PERs of 42.9x/26.0x, vs. its peak of 80x. 
Downside risks: 1) Competitive and pricing pressure; and 2) delays due to AI/HPC 
slowdown.

Chunghwa Precision Test Tech (CHPT, 6510 TT, TWD3,610, Buy [1]) 
• Aug revenue result: August revenue totalled TWD 638m (+3.9% MoM, +54.3% YoY), 
bringing 1-8M26 cumulative revenue to TWD 4.25bn (+33.14% YoY). Jul-Aug sales 
reached 66% of our 3Q26E revenue, tracking in line with our expectations. In response 
to Taiwan Stock Exchange (TWSE) inquiries following recent share price volatility, CHPT 
disclosed its August financial results. Single-month EPS came in at TWD 5.29 (net 
income of TWD 174m, +95% YoY), implying a quarterly EPS run-rate of TWD 15.87 (vs 
Daiwa estimate of TWD 16.08 and market consensus of TWD 17.52)

• Product mix & catalysts: Top-line momentum was broad-based across both the AI 
GPU and ASIC segments. Management highlighted ongoing footprint expansion and 
continued efforts to identify additional sites to scale manufacturing capacity. Moving 
forward, steady deliveries of AI GPU test boards, alongside the forthcoming volume 
ramp of custom AI ASICs, should underpin sequential revenue growth.

• Outlook: Our supply chain checks indicate CHPT is competing for three AI/HPC probe 
card turnkey programs, comprising a CPU ASIC, an AI ASIC program for a US 
automotive client, and another AI ASIC program for Taiwanese clients. The automaker 
qualification process is progressing smoothly, and CHPT plans to ship engineering 
probe cards for validation in November, with validation results expected by end-2026. 
Given the superior content value of probe card solutions versus board-only products, a 
successful qualification would provide incremental top-line upside into late-2026, while 
serving as a significant catalyst for 2027 revenue upgrades and valuation expansion.

• Valuation: We have a Buy (1) rating with a 12-month TP of TWD4,000, based on a 
target PER of 45x applied to our 4-quarter-forward EPS forecast. Based on our 
2027/28E EPS, the stock is currently trading at PERs of 30.2x/24.0x, vs. its peak of 68x. 
Downside risks: 1) project delays and utilisation risk; and 2) loss of market share.

AI semi capex, in particular for compute chips, should outgrow
overall AI capex in 2028. We add cross-checks on ASICs after
AVGO’s guidance and the Qualcomm/AWS AI ASIC
announcement.
Will 2028 be another growth year for AI semis? Our US internet analyst Brian
Nowak published the Morgan Stanley AI Guidebook: Navigating the Age of
Inference, and suggests 12% CSP capex growth into 2028, raising investor questions
about AI semi growth momentum. Based on our latest supply chain checks, we

introduce preliminary 2028 CoWoS/CoPoS/EMIB-T capacity assumptions. In
aggregate, we still see 50% capacity growth in 2028 (see Exhibit 1), implying
flattish infrastructure (e.g., the shell and AI DC) spending and moderate memory
pricing in 2028.
Larger chip size and more applications (CPU, networking) to drive further
growth in 2028: All these packaging technologies are 2.5D (compute and memory
chips on side to side) for AI/HPC chips, including AI GPU, XPU, CPU, and networking.

•CoWoS (wafer based): We still see some CoW expansion at TSMC. For oS, TSMC
may need more partners than ASE. Our checks suggest both Amkor and Xintec
(non-covered) will diversify oS production in the US and Taiwan, respectively.
• CoPoS (panel-based): This is a panel-level process. After converting to 12-inch
CoWoS wafer equivalents, we assume 5–10kwpm in 2028. The initial target project
should be Nvidia’s Feynman Ultra GPU.
• EMIB-T (substrate-based): Although a substrate-based technology, we convert it

to 12-inch CoWoS wafer-equivalent capacity as well. One EMIB-T substrate can
generate 16 pcs of a 9x reticle-size chip, around 4x the chips a CoWoS wafer can
generate. Our checks indicate ~40k–45k wafer-equivalent EMIB-T capacity in 2028,
producing at least 2mn of Humufish (9x reticle) substrates. 40k–45k wafer
equivalents also imply 10-15% share in 2.5D packaging.
Read-across from recent ASIC guidance at Broadcom and the Qualcomm/AWS
partnership

•According to Joe Moore’s report: Broadcom Inc.: Earnings solid; raising FY 28 EPS,
more specific FY27 revenue guidance is now US$115 bn, and that could double again
in FY28. Our CoWoS supply-chain checks already see an AVGO AI lab customer
booking ~15k–30k CoWoS wafers for 2027.
•Qualcomm announced its ASIC partnership with AWS, a key Alchip ASIC customer.
However, the press release points to inference, different from Alchip’s Trainium4,
which covers both training and inference. We therefore see limited impact to Alchip.

TSMC CoWoS and CoPoS
With continual booming demand in AI, we expect TSMC and its OSAT partners to expedite
capacity expansion plans for CoWoS. We expect TSMC's CoWoS capacity to further build
to 260kwpm in 2028 from 130/200kwpm in 2026/2027, with 2028's expansion mainly

focusing in Arizona's AP9/10 and potentially in AP7. TSMC is now conducting trial
production for CoPoS in Taoyuan. As long as the progress goes well, we expect TSMC to
start building its CoPoS capacity, with the location also likely to be in AP9/10 or AP7P3.

from 80kwpm by end-2027. For ASE/SPIL, we expect FoCoS+CoWoS capacity to rise from

and CoWoS-R. For Amkor, we expect CoWoS capacity to increase from 30kwpm at 2027-

How about  Intel EMIB capacity build?

### Page 4

M
Idea
4
For Intel, we see current average EMIB-M capacity being 110kwpm, which would slightly
decrease to 95kwpm in 2027 as it's converting some capacities toward EMIB-T. We see the
major customers of EMIB-M being Trainium3, upcoming Trainium 4 and its in-house server
CPU. Average EMIB-T capacity is around 5kwpm in 2026, which we expect to further scale
to 15-20kwpm in 2027 and 40-45kwpm in 2028. All of the capacity mentioned above is
converted for  12-inch wafer equivalent, which we believe would be much more easier to

communicate and compared with TSMC CoWoS capacity.
Although some small projects are ongoing, we still see MediaTek/Google Humufish
consuming most of the capacities during 2027-28. We maintain our view that Intel EMIB-T
could still  produce close to 3mn units of Humufish from late 2027-2028, by assuming 4-5
chips per wafer of EMIB-T.

2027 CoWoS allocation assumptions

Amkor) is slower than our initial expectation and customers' forecasts. Therefore, we now
see a slower ramp of AMD Venice CPU which should be the major customer of ASE and
Amkor CoWoS capacity.

CoWoS update (Exhibit 10):
• AMD's total 2027 CoWoS consumption increases 165% Y/Y to 345k:
° We expect AI GPU-related production to be all at TSMC CoWoS, while
MI455/450 would be the key focus for 2027, with minor production for MI500
series (chip code name Arcadia) in late 2027. Both MI455 and MI450 could
reach around 500-650k units respectively. We estimate total TSMC CoWoS-L
booking increases 200% Y/Y in 2027, to 210k.
° We see the non-TSMC camp, including ASE/SPIL and  Amkor, being mainly in

charge of Venice CPU, high-end PC CPU, and gaming GPU production.
Powertech was also planning for the production of Venice, but we now expect
it to focus on gaming GPU only, given the lower yield rate of panel level
packaging.
° However, given the slower production  ramp at OSAT,   we believe TSMC would

### Page 6

M
Idea
6
also need to support around 80k CoWoS-L for Venice production, partially
squeezing out the capacity of AI GPU build.
° We now expect Venice CPU's CoWoS booking to increase from 50k in 2026 to
210k in 2027, implying 4.4mn units of CPU production, lower than our initial
forecast of 5.6mn.
• Microsoft's Maia200 CoWoS booking increases from 1k in 2026e to 5k in 2027, we
estimate:
° We have been seeing upward revision demand of Microsoft Maia 200 into

2027; we believe the demand could reach around 5k of CoWoS in 2027,
suggesting around 100-150k chips.
• US AI Lab CoWoS booking could reach 15-30k in 2027:
° With the recent announcement of AI ASIC from one US AI lab, we expect its
CoWoS booking at TSMC to reach 15-30k in 2027, with support from
Broadcom.

Connecting Nvidia GPU shipments and rack shipments:
In Howard Kao’s rack shipment update, Greater China Technology Hardware: GB200 and
GB300 NVL72 Racks in August 2026 (9 Sep 2026), August racks recovered, implying
more Blackwell GPU consumption. His CY26 full-year rack forecast is now at 80-85K
racks. This is similar to our observation that KYEC continues final testing for meaningful
volumes of B-series GPUs in 3Q26.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.

## Document 093: 260911_nmr_panjit

Source PDF: all report pdf/260911_nmr_panjit.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-09-11

### Page 1

EQUITY: TECHNOLOGY
Panjit International   2481.TW 2481 TT
Global Markets Research
11 September 2026
Favorable pricing, product mix to elevate 2H26-2027F profit margins
New AI datacenter orders remain major growth driver
More positive on Panjit’s AI datacenter power MOSFET supply chain position 
Our recent market survey suggests sustained tight supply of select power discrete 
products in 2H26F, such as power/small signal MOSFET, and diodes. Future Electronics’ 
monthly report indicates lengthened lead times for many MOSFET and diode variants in 
Jul/Aug 2026, along with an uptrend in pricing (Fig 5). On the other hand, SDI’s (2351 TT, 
Buy) management indicated that IDM customers are placing more lead frame orders to the 
company with favorable pricing in 2H26E, in order to meet stronger AI-datacenter demand and 
to secure market share. As some products’ lead times have reached historical record levels 
(for e.g., select Schottky/switch diodes and LV/HV MOSFET lead times reached 52 weeks in 
Aug 2026, vs 20 weeks a year ago), and with limited capacity expansion among IDMs/ 
foundries in the near term, we expect the tight supply to continue in 2H26-1H27F. We also 
expect such a benign environment to be a tailwind for vendors to pass-through cost increases. 
We expect Panit’s 2H26-2027F revenue growth and margin expansion to sustain
Panjit’s 2Q26 revenue grew 16% q-q, better than management’s target, and was driven 
by improved demand across end markets. 2Q26 GM reached a 19-quarter high at 33.2% 
on a greater revenue scale, better product mix, and favorable pricing. We expect the 
revenue and profit margin q-q momentum to sustain in 2H26-2027F, as the company has 
secured more orders from the AI datacenter power discrete market, along with price hikes 
(mainly to pass-through rising fab manufacturing and material costs). We expect 3Q26-
4Q26F revenue to grow 10%/5% q-q, with AI datacenter related revenue contributing 12-14% 
total sales (vs 11% in 1H26). We also assume 2H26-2027F GM to reach 36.5%/37.3% (vs 
33.0% in 1H26) with higher AI and automotive sales exposure, better utilization rates across 
manufacturing sites, along with a third round of price hikes in 4Q26F. 
2026F/27F EPS raised by 3%/10%; TP increased to TWD214, implying 38.5% upside
We raise 2026F/27F EPS, as we assume contribution from more new AI datacenter 
orders mainly starting in 2H26-2027F, and expect a more favorable mix and pricing to 
form better margin profiles. Our TP of TWD214 is based on 25x 2027F EPS of TWD8.55 
(previous TP of TWD195 was based on 25x 2027F EPS of TWD7.79). The stock currently 
trades at 18x/16x 2027F/28F EPS.

### Page 3

Company profile
Panjit is a power semiconductor IDM headquartered in Taiwan. It currently manufactures and sells power discrete (including diodes, MOSFET, and
SIC), Power IC, and solar products. The company’s products are widely used in many end applications including industrial, green energy, consumer
electronics, automotive, computers, power supply, and communication.
Valuation Methodology
Our TP of TWD214 is based on 25x 2027F EPS of TWD8.55. We use 25x to derive our TP, vs historical P/E range of 18x since 2018 (The stock

traded in a range from 15-33x when the company was in earnings growth upcycle). The benchmark to the stock is TAIEX.
Risks that may impede the achievement of the target price
Downside risks: 1) global macroeconomic uncertainty that may drag demand for the company's products, especially auto and industrial; 2) slower-
than-expected supply chain inventory destocking; and 3) pricing competition from peers if global demand is worse than expected.
ESG

Panjit requires itself to uphold the business principles of “innovation, growth, responsibility, and sustainability” in the global semiconductor supply
chain in the face of unprecedented changes in the aspects of global environment and climate, economy and society. The company actively
promotes and continues to make improvement in environmental protection, employee safety and health, labor conditions, moral values, and the
maintenance of energy, striving to become a member of the green enterprises in the world while constantly strengthening ESG (the beauty of the

Environment, the goodness in society, and the truthfulness in governance) actions to respond to changes and challenges in the future with a high
level of dynamics.

Definition of Nomura Group's equity research rating system and sectors
The rating system is a relative system, indicating expected performance against a specific benchmark identified for each individual stock, 
subject to limited management discretion. An analyst’s target price is an assessment of the current intrinsic fair value of the stock based on an 
appropriate valuation methodology determined by the analyst. Valuation methodologies include, but are not limited to, discounted cash flow 
analysis, expected return on equity and multiple analysis. Analysts may also indicate expected absolute upside/downside relative to the stated 
target price, defined as (target price - current price)/current price.

SECTORS
A 'Bullish' stance, indicates that the analyst expects the sector to outperform the Benchmark during the next 12 months. A 'Neutral' stance, 
indicates that the analyst expects the sector to perform in line with the Benchmark during the next 12 months. A 'Bearish' stance, indicates that 
the analyst expects the sector to underperform the Benchmark during the next 12 months. Sectors that are labelled as 'Not rated' or shown as 
'N/A' are not assigned ratings. Benchmarks are as follows: United States: S&P 500; Europe: Dow Jones STOXX 600; Global Emerging 
Markets (ex-Asia): MSCI Emerging Markets ex-Asia. Japan/Asia ex-Japan: Sector ratings are not assigned.

Target Price
A Target Price, if discussed, indicates the analyst’s forecast for the share price with a 12-month time horizon, reflecting in part the analyst's 
estimates for the company's earnings. The achievement of any target price may be impeded by general market and macroeconomic trends, and 
by other risks related to the company or the market, and may not occur if the company's earnings differ from estimates.

Industry View In-Line
AI-driven specification upgrades are reshaping the economics of copper-clad
laminate (CCL), the core material in PCBs, and driving demand for higher-

performance copper foil and glass cloth. We expect the global CCL TAM to rise
from US$19B in 2025 to US$47B by 2030, implying a 20% CAGR (vs market
expectations at US$35-40B), with AI/data-center applications accounting for 90%
of incremental growth and nearly two-thirds of demand by decade-end.
Content, not units, is the key growth driver. We estimate rising material intensity
accounts for 84% of AI-related CCL growth, as next-generation compute and

networking platforms move to higher-layer-count PCBs, lower-loss CCL and
smoother HVLP copper foil. Our bottom-up model implies the HVLP4 market
grows from <US$50M in 2025 to US$2.8B by 2030, a 123% CAGR. Extending the
same framework further downstream, we forecast the global PCB TAM will rise
from US$58bn to US$135bn over 2025–30, an 18% CAGR.
Broadening the AI trade upstream. We think high-end CCL, HVLP copper foil and
advanced glass cloth have entered an earnings upcycle but have yet to reach full

earnings realization and peak-cycle conditions. AI-driven specification upgrades are
reducing the number of  qualified suppliers and supporting mix, pricing and margins,
while long qualification cycles, lower yields and equipment bottlenecks should slow
the supply response.
Morgan Stanley does and seeks to do business with
companies covered in Morgan Stanley Research. As a result,
investors should be aware that the firm may have a conflict of
interest that could affect the objectivity of Morgan Stanley

communications with a subject company, public appearances
and trading securities held by a research analyst account.
Key Takeaways
Long qualification cycles and lower production yields in advanced copper foil and
glass cloth should keep effective high-end CCL supply tight into 2028.
Our proprietary bottom-up analysis quantifies copper foil, CCL and PCB content
across Nvidia, AMD, TPU and Trainium platforms.
We introduce a scorecard comparing 16 PCB, substrate & materials suppliers on

six factors to distinguish structural winners from cyclical beneficiaries.
DDR4-like supply squeeze; standard E-glass pricing likely has further upside as
capacity shifts toward higher-value, more advanced grade cloth.
Initiate at OW on EMC (NT$8,400 PT), TUC (NT$2,700 PT), Co-Tech (NT$730
PT), and KB Laminates (HK$75 PT).

4
Executive Summary
Why read this report?
We provide a comprehensive understanding of what copper foil, and copper-clad
laminates are and how they fit into the PCB supply chain. In addition, we have built
a proprietary bottom-up model to model out the copper foil and copper-clad
laminates market (please reach out to us for the model).
We also broke down the copper foil, copper-clad laminate, and printed circuit
board content per AI system across the different Nvidia, AMD, TPU, and Trainium

architectures.
AI has completely overhauled the PCB supply chain, going all the way up to upstream
materials like CCL (key raw material used in PCB production), copper foil (used in CCL
production), as well as electronic glass cloth (used in CCL production) and others. ABF
substrates are also seeing similar tailwinds, but we have talked about that here, here, and
here. In this report, we focus primarily on CCL and copper foil, but also touch on glass

cloth.
We forecast the CCL market to grow at a 20% CAGR over 2025-30, to US$47B from
US$19B (2.5x). Unit and content growth both contribute. AI data center cluster
deployments lift volumes, while faster signal transmission speeds require upgraded CCL
to contain insertion loss and crosstalk, on top of a mix shift toward higher layer-count and
more complex PCBs.
Against this demand, we see high-end CCL as under-supplied. Only a handful of suppliers

globally are qualified for M8+ grades, including EMC, Panasonic, TUC, Doosan, and
Shengyi. Because CCL capacity is grade-agnostic in architecture, nameplate capacity
overstates effective high-grade supply, and M7/M8+ supply-demand is therefore difficult
to quantify with precision. The absence of a precise gap does not change the direction. We
expect high-end tightness to translate into firmer pricing and margin expansion for the
qualified incumbents.

In copper foil, we forecast the high-end HVLP4 market to reach US$2.8B by 2030, a
123% CAGR over 2025-2030, though off a de minimis base. HVLP4 (hyper very low
profile) foil is the enabling input for low-loss, high-speed CCL, and therefore a gating item
in the AI PCB supply chain.
Effective capacity rests with five suppliers today: Mitsui Kinzoku, Co-Tech, Furukawa,
Fukuda and Copper Foil Luxembourg at roughly 1,000t/month, with Mitsui alone at 41%

of 2026 capacity. Adding to that base is harder than nameplate suggests. Converting
HVLP2 lines is not one-for-one; the longer production cycle cuts effective output by  more
than 40%. New lines require 6-12 months to ramp, and sit behind delivery queues for
surface-treatment equipment concentrated among a small number of Japanese suppliers.
We model demand rising to 39.2kt in 2027 from 12.3kt in 2026 (+218% y/y), and further
growing to 52.0kt in 2028 (+33% y/y). That would leave the market in a deficit of 31% by

### Page 5

M
Foundation
Morgan Stanley Research 5
2027 and 20% by 2028. As such, we expect a handful of qualified producers to hold
meaningful pricing power over that window.
Our conclusion is that CCL makers that are able to secure the full input set of cloth, foil
and resin should hold pricing power over downstream customers.
As a result of our bottom-up analysis of CCL and its upstream materials, we expect the
PCB TAM to grow from US$58B in 2025 to US$135B by 2030, representing an 18%

CAGR.
Beyond discovery: Why high-end CCL stocks still have room
to run
We believe the market continues to underestimate the extent to which AI
infrastructure investment will expand end demand for copper-clad laminate. Our 2030
CCL total addressable market estimate of US$47B is meaningfully above market forecasts
of US$35-40B.
The key difference lies not only in our assumptions for AI server and data-center unit
growth, but also in the increasing material intensity of next-generation computing and

networking platforms. Higher layer counts, larger and more complex PCBs, faster data-
transmission requirements, and the growing use of premium low-loss and high-speed
materials should drive CCL content per system materially higher. This is all made possible
with more advanced copper foil  with tighter surface-profile, signal-integrity, and reliability
requirements.
Structural demand is supported by capex. The spending backdrop still has more than a

year of visible growth in it. Our  house view on cash cloud capex now stands at US$1.2T in
2026 (+104% y/y) and US$1.6T  in 2027 (+38% y/y) vs. consensus at US$1.39T for 2027.
This compares with buyside expectations of US$1.8T+ (including financial and operating
leases), suggesting there could still be meaningful room for further upward consensus
revisions.
CCL content is growing faster than AI-system units. The demand driver is a specification
ladder, not a single product cycle, and that distinction is what makes the cycle durable.

Layer counts on AI server motherboards are increasing with each generation to support
additional routing space for more signals as AI boards become more complex, while rising
signal and thermal demands also support the adoption of higher grade CCLs. Crucially,
each rung multiplies rather than adds, because moving up a grade changes the resin
chemistry, the copper-foil profile and the glass-cloth grade simultaneously. Content per
rack can therefore keep rising even if unit shipment growth moderates. The continuing

migration toward lower-loss grades is another reason the earnings opportunity may have
further to run.
