Co-Tech Development: Initiate at Overweight, Price Target
of NT$730
Company overview
Co-Tech Development Corporation (Co-Tech) was established in May 1998  to manufacture
electrodeposited (ED) copper foil for the copper-clad laminate (CCL) and printed circuit
board (PCB) industries. Plant 1 entered mass production in 2000 and Plant 2 followed in
2002,. Co-Tech registered on Taiwan's emerging stock board in 2007 and was formally

listed on the Taiwan OTC market in 2010; it changed its name to Co-Tech Development
Corporation in 2014.
Headquarters and the R&D centre are in Neihu Technology Park, Taipei, and all
manufacturing sits at a single site in Yunlin County, Taiwan, where Plant 1 and Plant 2
operate and Plant 3 is under construction. The current wholly-Taiwan manufacturing
footprint is a point of difference versus most peers, which carry China or Southeast Asia
capacity; it removes China tariff/localization exposure but concentrates operational risk in

one site.
Key products
Copper foil is the conductive layer of a CCL: the laminator bonds foil onto resin-
impregnated glass cloth to form the core, so foil surface roughness (Rz) sets the floor on
insertion loss at high frequency. Co-Tech's ladder runs from LP310/HG310 (standard to
mid-loss, outer layer) through the RT/RG reverse-treated family (RG311 at Rz ≤2.3µm,
RG312 at ≤2.1µm, and the more advanced RG313) into the HVLP series (VG410 at ≤1.5µm,

VL411 at ≤1.0µm, plus the more advanced PF51N (HVLP4+) and PF511 (HVLP5)).
Product mix by grade: Co-Tech fully stopped supplying conventional HTE and standard
RTF in 3Q26, redirecting all capacity to the RG (advanced RTF) and HVLP series. Co-Tech
estimates its RG series holds over dominant share of the general server market, while
HVLP is more aimed at AI servers and high-end switches. Today, we estimate the RG family
remains Co-Tech single largest revenue line at 73%, while HVLP revenue mix is at 14% in

2026. We expect Co-Tech HVLP3+ mix to reach 14%/34%/46% of sales in
2026/2027/2028, primarily driven by AI server boards and 800G/1.6T switches. This mix
shift plus processing-fee hikes should lift Co-Tech's gross margin from 22% in 2025 to
30%/40%/47% in 2026/2027/2028, on our estimates.

Profitability for copper foil manufacturers is ultimately set by the processing fee rather
than the copper price, since the metal cost is passed through to customers. Copper prices
are nonetheless a useful read on demand conditions. As  Exhibit 71 shows, periods of
strong electronics demand have coincided with firmer processing fees and expanding
gross margins at Co-Tech, typically with a one- to two-quarter lag before the benefit

reaches the P&L.

Key competitors
The competitive set varies by grade. In commodity HTE and standard foil, the volume
suppliers are Chinese producers plus Chang Chun (unlisted) and Nan Ya Plastics (1303.TW,

not covered). This is no longer Co-Tech's market.
Co-Tech primarily competes in advanced RTF and HVLP3+. Mitsui Kinzoku (5706.T,
covered by Yu Shirakawa) is the clear technology and capacity leader in HVLP3+, followed
by Furukawa Electric (5801.T, also covered by Yu Shirakawa), Co-Tech, Fukuda (unlisted),
Circuit Foil Luxembourg (CFL, unlisted) and Lotte Energy  Materials (020150.KS, covered
by Ryan Kim). The second tier suppliers are mainly China-based, such as Tongguan Copper

Foil (301217.SZ, not covered), Jiujiang Defu Technology (301511.SZ, not covered), Jiangxi
Copper (600362.SS, covered by Rachel Zhang) and Kingboard Laminates, etc.

68
On our estimates (Exhibit 73& Exhibit 74), Mitsui Kinzoku, Co-Tech, Furukawa, Fukuda
and CFL together account for 88%/78%/76% of global HVLP4+ copper foil capacity in
2026/2027/2028; Mitsui Kinzoku will remain the share leader for the most high-end
grades, but Co-Tech is a close follower, with supply share expected to reach 20%+ by
2028, on our estimates.

Key customers

Co-Tech sits one step further upstream and sells circuit foils to CCL (PCB inner layers) and
PCB makers (PCB outer layers). The customer base is led by the Taiwan laminators —
Elite Material (2383.TW), ITEQ (6213.TW, not covered) and TUC (6274.TWO), but Co-Tech
also supplies to Shengyi Tech and Doosan. Because demand reaches Co-Tech through the
laminators, end-platform exposure is indirect.
What are the key risks?
• AI demand is structural but volatile: Hardware/CCL orders can swing with delays,

bottlenecks and inventory digestion.
• Qualification creates an edge, not permanence: Incumbents benefit, but each
platform transition can reopen competition.
• Competition is rising: Chinese entrants could pressure share, pricing and margins,
even initially via second-source positions.
• Other materials are constrained: Advanced glass cloth shortages could also cap
copper foil and CCL shipments.
• CPO is a longer-term risk: It could reduce demand for premium copper foil grades,

but is unlikely to materially affect 2026 tightness.
Price target and valuation methodology
Our price target for Co-Tech is NT$730: This implies 39x CY27 or 23x CY28 P/E. Our
price target is also our base case scenario value. It is derived from our residual income (RI)

### Page 69

M
Foundation
Morgan Stanley Research 69
model (similar to the rest of our tech hardware universe).
To calculate the fair value of the stock, we use the company's beginning equity plus the
present value of all expected residual income of earnings in excess of cost of capital. The
RI is positive when ROAE is above the cost of capital and negative when it is below the
cost of capital. Our key assumptions include a cost of equity of 9.3% (beta of 1.0, equity

risk premium of 8.7%, and risk-free rate of 1.0%), a medium-term growth rate of 13% and a
terminal growth rate of 3%.
The 23x CY28 P/E implied by our price target is slightly above the past 3Y average  of 20x.
We deem this valuation as fair, because Co-Tech is benefitting from an earnings growth
cycle fueled by stronger AI server/networking CCL demand, Co-Tech's continued mix
improvement (from HTE + RTF (RG) copper foils to advanced RTF (RG) + HVLP3+ copper

foils) and continued  processing fee adjustments, which supports margin expansion from
21.8% in 2025 to 47.2% by 2028 and a EPS CAGR of 96% over 2025-2028, on our
estimates. The stock is trading at just 16.4x CY28 P/E, below Co-Tech's past 3Y average
20x. Thus, we view the risk reward as attractive.
Bull case value NT$1,200: In this scenario, we assume high-end servers and switches see
significantly stronger growth in 2027-28, driven by faster-than-expected new data center

build-outs and smooth ramp of new generations of AI/networking chips; we also assume
copper foil demand from key satellite customer will be stronger than expected. As a
result, we assume Co-Tech's copper foil demand from the aforementioned applications to
remain robust and its product mix to see continued improvement from the adoption of
HVLP4+ copper foils from more applications. Under this scenario, our bull case value
implies 38x 2028e EPS.
Bear case value NT$300: In this scenario, we assume high-end servers and switches see

significantly weaker demand in 2027-28, driven by  slowdown in  new data center build-
outs and delayed ramps of new generations of AI/networking chips; we also assume
copper foil demand from key satellite customer will be stagnant. As a result, we assume
Co-Tech's copper foil demand from the aforementioned applications to become sluggish
and its customers' migration to the HVLP4+ platform to be put on hold. Under this
scenario, our bear case value implies 9x 2028e EPS.

KB Laminates (1888.HK): Initiate at Overweight, Price Target
of HK$75
Company overview
Kingboard Laminates (KB Laminates) is the laminates arm of the Kingboard Group, spun
out of Kingboard Holdings (0148.HK) and listed on the Main Board of the Hong Kong
Stock Exchange in December 2006. The operating history predates the listing by nearly
two decades. The group built its first laminate plant in Shenzhen in 1988 and began

producing paper laminates in 1989, then expanded horizontally into glass epoxy and
flame-retardant paper laminates and vertically into copper foil, glass yarn, glass fabric,
bleached kraft paper and epoxy resin, with the Fogang copper foil operation itself spun off
and listed in Singapore in 1999 as Kingboard Copper Foil. That upstream ownership
(vertical integration) is the defining feature of the business and the clearest contrast with
most other CCL suppliers globally. Kingboard self-supplies most of its critical inputs and

competes on cost, scale and supply security, whereas its competitors buy its inputs and
compete on specification.
KB Laminates positions itself as the world's largest CCL producer by volume, a position it
dates to the mid-2000s.  Kingboard Laminates remains controlled through the parent,
reports across the Laminates, Properties and Investments segments, and is headquartered
in Sha Tin, Hong Kong.
Key products
KB Laminates mainly reports across three business segments:

• Laminates
• Properties
• Investments
In 2025, laminates accounted for 99% of revenue and 98% of operating profits.
• Within the laminates business, we believe the majority of revenue comes from CCL
(80-90%), while the rest comes from upstream materials such as copper foil, glass
cloth, etc.
Capacity
The laminate segment's capacity footprint is concentrated in China. In Guangdong, the
core sites are Fogang in Qingyuan City (both glass epoxy and paper laminate plants, the

historical heart of the business), Shixing County in Shaoguan, Jiangmen in the Jianghai high-
tech zone, and Guangzhou. In Jiangsu, there is Kunshan in the economic and technological
development zone, plus Jiangyin for composite material.
Copper foil capacity mainly sits at Fogang and Lianzhou in Guangdong, with a new
21,000-tonne plant for high-frequency and low-loss foil starting mid-2027 in Fogang.

### Page 76

M
Foundation
76
Glass yarn and cloth capacity mainly run through Qingyuan, Lianzhou and Panyu Nansha,
with the first 500-tonne low-Dk yarn kiln at Qingyuan commissioned in 1H25, a six-kiln
specialty yarn factory planned in the Qingyuan park, and a Shaoguan project carrying
70,000 tonnes of yarn and 100M meters of cloth from 2H26.
Bleached kraft paper is made at Taicang in Jiangsu and Qingyuan.

Key competitors
The competitive landscape varies significantly by product grade. In commodity FR-4,
the largest volume suppliers include China’s Shengyi Technology and Taiwan’s Nan Ya
Plastics, with the top five producers accounting for more than 60% of the global CCL
market. This remains Kingboard Laminates’ core market today.
Kingboard is now expanding into M6-and-above high-speed, low-loss CCL. The first

phase of its new capacity is scheduled to come online in 1H27, targeting 350,000 sheets
per month, followed by a second phase of an additional 350,000 sheets per month in
1H28. In this higher-end segment, Kingboard will compete with Elite Material, Taiwan
Union Technology, ITEQ, Panasonic, Doosan Electro-Materials, and Shengyi Technology.
Key customers
Kingboard Laminates sells its CCL directly to PCB fabricators, serving both independent

### Page 77

M
Foundation
Morgan Stanley Research 77
customers and affiliated manufacturers within the broader Kingboard Group. The group’s
downstream PCB operations include uo. These affiliated customers provide a captive
outlet for part of Kingboard’s production and allow closer coordination on material
development, qualification, and production planning. However, the laminates business
remains predominantly externally focused. In 2025, the segment generated HK$16.9

billion in external revenue and HK$3.8 billion in intersegment revenue, implying that
external sales accounted for approximately 82% of the total. We believe KB Laminates’
external PCB customer base includes TTM Technologies, Tripod Technology, Meiko
Electronics, VGT, and Kinwong, among others.
Kingboard’s vertical integration also extends upstream into several of the key raw
materials used in CCL production, including copper foil, electronic fiberglass yarn and

cloth, epoxy resin, and paper-based materials. These operations primarily support the
group’s own laminate manufacturing, giving Kingboard greater control over raw-material
availability, cost, quality, and production scheduling. Surplus output is sold to third-party
CCL manufacturers, providing an additional revenue stream, although the main strategic
purpose of these assets is to support Kingboard’s internal supply chain. This structure is
particularly valuable during periods of tight raw-material supply, when less-integrated

competitors may face higher input costs or production constraints. We estimate that KB
Laminates sells 20% of its upstream materials to third parties. This includes glass cloth
and copper foil supplied to external CCL manufacturers (such as EMC, Shengyi, Panasonic,
Doosan, etc), as well as glass yarn sold to independent glass-cloth weavers such as
Fulltech, amongst others.
Pricing tailwind for standard grade electronic cloth
We have seen ASP for standard E-Glass cloth rising, and we think there is more to come.

Strong demand for higher-grade, more advanced cloth is causing more suppliers to shift
capacity over, and a result of that shift, is causing constraints on standard grade cloth,
thus creating this tailwind. This is one of the reasons why KB Laminates saw strong margin
improvement in its 1H26 results.
Electronic fiberglass earnings inflection likely to accelerate in 2H26: According to KB
Laminates’ 2025 annual report, profit contribution from the electronic fiberglass yarn and

fabric business exceeded HK$600M in CY25, representing 70% y/y growth. This implies a
CY24 contribution of HK$350M, or approximately 27% of group profit. The segment’s
contribution increased further to HK$1.0B in 1H26 (1H26 interim results), equivalent to
approximately 35% of total group profit, versus approximately 25% in CY25.
We expect the earnings contribution from electronic fiberglass to strengthen further in
2H26. KB  Laminates implemented price increases in each month of 1H26, and the

associated earnings benefit typically flows through with a lag as existing contracts and
inventory are repriced. As a result, we believe several of the price increases announced
during 1H26 will only be fully reflected in 2H26 earnings. Additional price increases in
early July and late August should provide further support into the remainder of the year.
This pricing momentum may help explain management’s disclosure that July profit reached
HK$1.1B, a particularly strong result relative to the HK$2.9B reported for the entirety of

1H26. While monthly earnings may be volatile and July’s performance should not
necessarily be extrapolated in full, the current run rate suggests that 2H26 profit could

### Page 78

M
Foundation
78
exceed HK$5.6B, with electronic fiberglass likely accounting for an increasing share of the
group’s earnings mix.

What are the key risks?
• AI demand is structural but volatile: Hardware/CCL orders can swing with delays,
bottlenecks and inventory digestion.
• Qualification creates an edge, not permanence: Incumbents benefit, but each
platform transition can reopen competition.
• Competition is rising: Other new Chinese entrants could pressure share, pricing

and margins.
• Upstream materials are constrained: Advanced glass cloth and HVLP copper foil
shortages support pricing but can cap shipments.
• CPO is a longer-term risk: It could reduce demand for premium CCL grades, but is
unlikely to materially affect 2026 tightness.

Morgan Stanley Research 79
Price target and valuation methodology
Our price target for KB Laminates is HK$75: This implies 14x CY28 P/E. Our price  target
is also our base case scenario value. It is derived from our residual income (RI)  model
(similar to the rest of our tech hardware universe).
To calculate the fair value of the  stock, we use the company's beginning equity plus the
present value of all expected  residual income of earnings in excess of cost of capital. The

RI is positive when ROAE is  above the cost of capital and negative when it is below the
cost of capital. Our key assumptions include a cost of equity of 9.7% (beta of 1.0, equity
risk premium of 8.7%, and risk-free rate of 1.0%), a medium-term growth rate of 8% and a
terminal growth rate of 3%.
The 14x CY28 P/E implied by our price target is slightly above the past 3Y average  of
12.8x. We deem this valuation as fair, because KB Laminates is benefitting from an

earnings growth cycle fueled by price hikes of its FR4 CCLs and glass cloth, as well as its
gradual penetration into the higher-end CCL material market, which supports margin
expansion from 19.6% in 2025 to 41.7% by 2028 and a EPS CAGR of 91% over 2025-2028,
on our estimates. That said, we believe the company remains more of a cyclical play
compared to its Taiwanese peers including EMC and TUC. Thus, we view the 1x re-rating
vs. its past 3Y P/E average as fair. The stock is trading at just 8.5x CY28 P/E, below KB's

past 3Y average of 13x. Therefore, we view the risk reward as attractive.
Bull case value HK$115: In this scenario, we assume demand for general server,
networking switches, and AI servers sees significantly stronger growth in 2026-28, driven
by replacement and new data center builds. In addition, we also assume KB Laminates is
able to secure and take share in the high-speed, low-loss CCL market (M6+ grade CCLs),
while also able to continue to hike price well into 2027 with no signs of pricing declines

into 2028. Under this scenario, our bull case value implies 21.3x CY28 P/E.
Bear case value HK$33: In this scenario, we assume demand for general server,
networking switches, and AI servers declines vs our current expectations of growth, in
2027-28. And therefore KB Laminates is not able to take share in the high-speed, low-loss
CCL market, as leading incumbents compete on price to keep utilization rates full. We also
assume KB Laminates will have to start lowering prices for standard grade CCLs. Under

this scenario, our bear case value implies 6.1x CY28 P/E.

Morgan Stanley Research 91
Copper-Clad Laminates (CCL) Primer

Introduction: What are copper-clad laminates (CCL)?
CCL is the major determinant foundation of all PCBs (printed circuit boards). It 
dictates signal integrity, reliability, manufacturability, and cost across all PCBs, 
ranging from consumer electronics to automotive to AI infrastructure hardware 
(server, storage, networking, etc). CCL is a composite. 
In the hierarchy of electronics supply chain, CCL serves as the fundamental building block 
for printed circuit boards (PCBs). It is a composite material created by reinforcing a resin 
matrix with a filler (typically fiberglass cloth) and cladding it with electrolytic copper foil 
on one or both sides. CCL is a critical proxy for downstream demand in high-growth 
sectors such as AI servers, 5G infrastructure and automotive electronics. 
Core composition and manufacturing
The performance of a CCL is dictated by its three primary components:
• Copper foil: This is the conductive layer that is etched to create electrical 
pathways for signals and currents. The industry is shifting toward ultra-low-profile 
copper foils for high-speed or high-frequency CCLs to minimize signal loss (ie. 
HVLP3+). 
• Reinforcement materials: In most cases, electronic-grade fiberglass cloth serves 
as the reinforcement material in a CCL, which provides mechanical strength and 
dimensional stability.
• Resin system: This is the "glue" of the laminate and mainly where the CCL 
suppliers differentiate from each other. In some high-speed applications, standard 
FR-4 (epoxy) is replaced by specialized resins like polytetrafluoroethylene (PTFE) 
or polyphenylene ether (PPE) to achieve lower dielectric constant (Dk) and 
dissipation factor (Df).

### Page 92

M
Foundation
92
Exhibit 94: Cross-section of a CCL
Source: Doosan.
CCL powerhouses: Who’s leading the market?
There are three key specifications that determine a CCL supplier's tier in the supply chain:
1. Dielectric constant (Dk) measures the ability of the material to store electrical
energy. Lower Dk allows for faster signal transmission.
2. Dissipation factor (Df) represents the energy "lost" as heat. For AI and high-
frequency applications, ultra-low loss materials, which have very low Df, are the

common standard and command higher ASPs.
3. Glass transition temperature (Tg) is the temperature at which the material shifts
from a rigid to a rubbery state. High-Tg materials are essential for reliability in
heat-intensive environments like within servers.
The global CCL market is led by KB Laminates and Shengyi Technology in China; Elite
Materials (EMC), Nan Ya, ITEQ, TUC, and Chang Chun in Taiwan; Panasonic in Japan;
Doosan in Korea; and Isola in the US. KB Laminates and Shengyi together account for

roughly one-third of global CCL capacity, but both remain heavily weighted toward
conventional grade CCL. We estimate that less than 5% of KB Laminates’ capacity is
allocated to M6+ products, compared with approximately 5–10% for Shengyi.
In high-speed CCL, EMC, TUC, and Doosan have gained share from Panasonic in recent
years. The shift began in servers and high-end networking around 2020 and accelerated
from 2023 as these suppliers secured early qualifications for AI-related reference designs.

We estimate that EMC, TUC, Doosan, and Panasonic each allocate more than 50% of their
capacity to M6+ grades.

Why higher-grade CCL is disproportionately harder to 
produce
Advancing CCL performance is not a matter of upgrading a single input — it requires 
simultaneously re-engineering copper foil, resin, and glass fiber, each of which becomes 
progressively harder to produce and integrate as electrical requirements tighten.
Resin –  two competing requirements in one material system. As copper foil surfaces are 
smoothed to reduce signal loss, resin formulation must solve two problems at once rather 
than one. Lower Dk/Df increasingly requires low-polarity resin chemistries, but the same 
low polarity that improves electrical performance also reduces the resin's natural ability 
to bond to copper and glass. With the mechanical grip previously provided by rough foil

### Page 94

M
Foundation
94
no longer available, adhesion must be re-engineered from scratch — through coupling
agents, functional end-groups, and in some cases metallic interlayers. Because these two
requirements pull in opposite directions within the same formulation, qualifying a new
resin/foil combination at HVLP3/HVLP4 level is an inherently slow, iterative process.
Glass fiber –  trading a well-understood material for a harder one. The same dynamic
applies to glass fiber reinforcement. Lowering Dk/Df means moving away from E-glass —

a chemically well-behaved, decades-optimized material — toward compositions that are
either harder to melt and draw into fiber consistently (NE-/NER-glass) or harder to handle
without breakage (quartz/Q-glass). In both cases, the sizing treatment that bonds glass to
resin must also be redeveloped rather than carried over from lower grades.
Lamination –  tighter process windows, less margin for error. These upstream changes
converge at the CCL manufacturer's lamination step. Bonding a low-adhesion foil to resin

using the same temperature, pressure, and cure-time profile used for standard foil risks
voids or delamination. Producers must instead adjust lamination profiles and use resin
systems specifically formulated for compatibility with low-profile foil — reinforcing why
advancing an M-grade tier requires changing resin, foil, and glass together, rather than
substituting a single component.
Net effect, structurally lower yields and a narrower supplier base. The cumulative result

of these compounding constraints is lower production yields, longer and more iterative
qualification cycles, and a small pool of suppliers capable of meeting spec reliably at scale.
This explains both the substantial cost premium commanded by higher-grade CCL and the
persistent lag between capacity additions and demand: the constraint is not simply
building more production lines, but building lines capable of consistently meeting a
materially tighter specification.

Source: Morgan Stanley Research.

The high bar for CCL qualification

Qualification for high-grade CCL, typically M7 and above, can take 12 to 18 months with
major customers such as Nvidia, AWS, Google, and Meta. The process is demanding
because it evaluates more than whether the laminate meets its datasheet specifications. A
supplier’s internal quality control typically measures properties such as Dk/Df, peel
strength, and Tg on flat coupons from a controlled sample batch. Customer qualification,
by contrast, tests the material after it has been drilled, plated, laminated, and built into

the customer’s actual board design using the designated PCB fabricator’s process.
Performance must also remain consistent across multiple production lots, rather than a
single optimized sample.
This distinction matters because signal integrity and reliability in a finished board can
differ materially from what flat-laminate testing suggests. Via-stub reflections, glass-
weave skew on the actual trace geometry, and voiding caused by narrow lamination

windows may only emerge during board-level testing. Customers also apply tighter,
application-specific acceptance criteria than standard industry specifications, reflecting the
high cost of a field failure in an AI server.
CCL capacity is fungible, not equivalent
CCL capacity is fungible mainly because the manufacturing process is essentially
grade-agnostic. Whatever the grade, you are doing the same three things:
1. impregnating woven glass cloth with resin on a treater to make prepreg,

2. stacking that prepreg with copper foil, and curing the stack under heat and
pressure in a lamination press,
3. then trimming and inspecting.
A standard FR-4 line and a low-loss line are the same machines in the same sequence.
What differs is the recipe, not the architecture.
That means switching grades is closer to a bill-of-materials swap than a capital project.
You change the resin system (epoxy for FR-4, PPE/PPO or hydrocarbon blends for low-

### Page 96

M
Foundation
96
loss, higher-Tg or halogen-free chemistries for others), the glass cloth (E-glass versus low-
Dk NE- or Q-glass), and the copper foil (standard versus reverse-treat or HVLP/ULP for
high-frequency loss budgets). You also retune press profiles, cure temperature, and
vacuum. All of that lives in the process recipe and the purchasing order book, which is why
CCL suppliers can and do reallocate lines toward whatever grade is earning the best

profit.
The fungibility is asymmetric, though, and this matters. A line qualified for high-end
material can trivially drop down and run commodity FR-4, despite it being over-qualified
for the job. However, the reverse is not as straightforward. Upgrading a commodity line
often requires better vacuum lamination, tighter thickness and resin-content control,
cleaner handling to avoid particulate defects, and frequently a dedicated treater to
prevent resin cross-contamination. So capacity flows downhill freely, and requires capex

and time going uphill.
The most important consequence for output is that fungible does not mean
equivalent. High-end grades consume more line time per unit of area: longer and hotter
press cycles, thinner cores that mean more panels for the same copper area, more
rigorous AOI and electrical testing, and materially lower first-pass yield during ramp. The
same physical asset therefore produces meaningfully fewer square meters per month when
it is running ultra-low-loss material than when it is running consumer FR-4, the penalty is

often on the order of tens of percent rather than a rounding error.
Layered on top is a commercial friction that is slower than the engineering one. CCL is
qualified into an OEM's approved-vendor list at the level of a specific grade produced on a
specific line at a specific site, with reliability testing behind it. Requalifying a line typically
takes quarters, not weeks, and PCB fabricators themselves have to requalify their own
process on the new laminate. So the theoretical swing capacity in an industry model is real

but arrives with a lag, and it arrives unevenly across customers.
Fungibility at the CCL step also does not fix a bottleneck sitting upstream of it. The
binding constraints in a high-end cycle are usually low-Dk glass cloth, HVLP copper foil,
and specialty resin, inputs with concentrated supply, long qualification cycles, and 12- to
24-month capacity lead times. A producer can reallocate every press it owns toward high-
end product and still be capped by how much low-Dk cloth it can source. Adding CCL

capacity into that situation raises utilization risk without raising effective supply.
Putting it altogether: Because capacity is fungible, a demand pull in one grade does not
sit contained within that grade, it propagates. Reallocation toward high-end material
shrinks industry area output measured in square meters while expanding revenue and
gross-profit capacity, since price per square meter for advanced grades runs multiples of
commodity. It simultaneously crowds out lower grades, which is why tightness that

originates in AI server boards shows up as lead-time extension and price increases in
unrelated end markets like automotive and consumer PCB. From a modeling perspective,
area-based nameplate capacity can overstate effective supply during a product-mix shift.
Press-hours, or area-equivalent capacity adjusted for grade-specific cycle times and yields,
provide a better measure, although these inputs are company-specific and generally
confidential.
Differences and similarities with this cycle vs. 2020-2022

### Page 97

M
Foundation
Morgan Stanley Research 97
The major differences
Structural versus pull-forward demand: AI infrastructure is a multi-year investment
cycle; pandemic demand was driven by temporary work-from-home and consumer-
electronics purchases.
This cycle is narrow and content-led, not broad and anonymous. 2021 was every
consumer device shipping at once, reaching CCL through two layers of distributor and PCB
inventory. This comes from a concentrated handful of hyperscalers and accelerator

roadmaps that specify the grade directly: far better visibility, far worse concentration.
Capacity only counts if it is qualified. In 2021 any line relieved the shortage. Now it has
to be a qualified position at grade (M8, M8.5, M9) with a 12-18 month qualification cycle,
so the 2021 response of adding standard capacity does nothing for the tight part of the
market.
The constraint sits further upstream and clears more slowly. Copper, resin and E-glass

were tight in 2021 and cleared inside roughly 18 months. Low-Dk and HVLP foil are near-
monopoly inputs with new capacity in 2H27 at the earliest, behind CCL equipment on two-
year lead times.
Margins are dispersing rather than rising together. The pandemic lifted everyone's
utilization. Profitability now turns on qualified mix and glass fabric allocation, which
makes this a stock selection cycle rather than a sector one.
The similarities
Demand is again outrunning available supply in the most constrained product

categories.
Strong demand visibility encourages capacity expansion by both CCL producers and
upstream material suppliers.
Capacity takes time to respond; tightness is cascading into grades unrelated to the
driver. Standard FR-4 inflated on generic input shortages then and is being squeezed by AI
allocation now. Non-driver end markets pay for it, and that slice of the price gain reversed
first last time.
Higher-end suppliers benefit disproportionately through better product mix, utilization

and margins.
If it breaks, it breaks the same way. The cycle could ultimately overshoot, with
aggressive investment and inventory building creating future oversupply and price
pressure. A platform slip or de-spec would unwind through the 2H22 route: standard
grades destock first, prices follow inputs down, and CCL feels it before the rest of the
chain because it sits upstream of the PCB.

### Page 98

M
Foundation
98
Copper Foil Primer
What is electro-deposited copper foil?
Electro-deposited (ED), or electrolytic,  copper foils are mainly used in lithium batteries
(battery foil) and PCB/CCLs (circuit foil). The production process of electrolytic copper foil
primarily involves dissolving copper into an electrolyte, depositing it as a very thin
continuous sheet on a rotating drum, peeling it from the drum, surface-treat it and slit   it
to the customer's required width. The basic process platforms for circuit foil and battery

foil are similar, but the downstream requirements are pretty different.
• Battery foil is coated with anode material and transports electrons during charging
and discharging.
• Circuit foil is laminated to resin and glass fabric to make CCL, and then etched into
conductive tracks for signals and currents.
Therefore, a circuit foil producer optimizes thickness, surface profile, adhesion and  signal
transmission. For high-speed/high-frequency applications, the focus shifts from simple

conductivity to signal integrity and adhesion physics. As signal frequencies increase, the
"skin effect" (Exhibit 101) becomes the dominant challenge. At high frequencies, electrons
no longer 'flow' through the center of copper but instead crowd toward the surface, which
means the signal has to travel a longer, jagged physical distance if the surface is rough
(resembling a mountain range under microscope), leading to significant signal loss.
Exhibit 100:Copper foil by production process and application

Source: Morgan Stanley Research.

### Page 99

M
Foundation
Morgan Stanley Research 99
Exhibit 101:Copper foil skin effect
Source: Morgan Stanley Research.
The PCB/CCL BoM typically specifies one of the following grades based on the required
profile, which is usually surface roughness, measured as Rz.
• HTE (High Temperature Elongation): The standard product. Good for 1-2L PCBs but
too rough for high-speed PCBs/CCLs.
• RTF (Reversed Treated Foil): The rough side is flipped to face the bonding layer,

while the signal-carrying side is smoother. This is a cost-effective middle ground,
but the surface roughness typically cannot meet the requirements of very high-
speed PCBs/CCLs.
• VLP (Very Low Profile): This is significantly smoother than the previous two but
also more expensive.
• HVLP (Hyper Very Low Profile): This is the standard for AI server and high-end
networking PCBs/CCLs, enabling sharp reduction of signal attenuation.
Key Technical Trade-offs: Rough-surfaced copper anchors into the resin system like a hook.

When you move to HVLP copper foil, the physical bond weakens, making adhesion
difficult. CCL suppliers must use chemical treatments to ensure the copper does not de-
laminate during soldering; the mastery of such treatment is a key testament to a CCL
supplier's manufacturing capability.

hyper-very-low-profile. The surface roughness is measured as Rz.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

### Page 1

ab 15 September 2026
Global Research
First Read
Voltronic Power
UBS Taiwan Summit 2026 key takeaways:
Earnings growth hinges on UPS/HVDC deliveries
Sales turning to growth in H226; sales mix change driving margin expansion
Management indicated bookings and shipments remained solid in July-Aug despite
temporary PCB shortages, and it expects a sales turnaround to growth in H226. Inverter
sales could stay weak on competition and Voltronic's product portfolio adjustments

(reducing low-margin products). Meanwhile, increasing outsourcing by brands will
support UPS sales growth. Management expects HVDC products to start contributing in
2027. Voltronic added a third customer for HVDC products. We think a higher sales
portion from UPS/HVDC products and inverter product adjustments could bring GM
expansion in 2027.
UPS growth intact;  HVDC and PDU represent the next legs of growth
The company expects sales to resume growth by single digits  in H226  and expects >10%

YoY growth in 2027. Management remains cautious about solar  inverters due to intense
competition from Chinese peers and expects inverter sales to decline as Voltronic adjusts
its product portfolio, focusing more on commercial solar  and premium products. For
2027, management expects offline UPS to grow 0-5%, supported by outsourcing
opportunities and emerging market demand, which could offset notebook weakness.
High-power UPS could account for an increasingly higher sales portion, driven by

accelerating outsourcing by tier 1 customers. For HVDC, management highlighted it
obtained one more client. HVDC product progress is on schedule, with 800VDC power
shelf sampling expected by year-end, a pilot run in H127 and mass production in H227.
The company is preparing testing and production equipment at factories in Taiwan and
China. Over the longer term, management also sees opportunities in PDUs, a market it
estimates at US$5bn in 2025, with a 12-15% sales CAGR over the next 5-8 years.

Margin  improvement on product mix optimisation and higher prices
Voltronic has implemented two UPS price increases this year to offset higher raw
material costs, which it expects to be reflected on its P&L starting in Sept 2026. For solar
inverters, management plans to focus on higher power and premium products with a
GM above 20%, while reducing low-power products with a GM at or below 20% over
the next two to three quarters, which could support GM expansion. In upcoming

quarters, solar inverter products may shrink to <20% of the total sales mix, while higher-
margin businesses such as high-power UPS and HVDC products continue to ramp. We
think the sales mix change will expand margins in 2027.
Valuation: Maintain Buy rating and PT of NT$1,128
We maintain our Buy rating and price target of NT$1,128, based on sum-of-the-parts
analysis, applying 28x/23x/24x 2027E PE for UPS/inverter/PSU business, respectively.

Company Description
Founded in 2008, Voltronic is a leading ODM for uninterruptible power supply (UPS) and
inverters. Its major customers include Schneider, Vertiv, and Riello. Voltronic is headquartered
in Taiwan and has production plants in mainland China, Vietnam, and Taiwan.
Valuation Method and Risk Statement

We base our price target on sum of the parts.
We believe the key risks for Voltronic include: 1) a slower-than-expected outsourcing trend
from leading branded UPS companies; 2) a faster-than-expected ramp-up of the electricity
grid in developing countries resulting in lower-than-expected demand for off-grid solar
systems; 3) FX risks; and 4) rising raw material prices.
Key industry-specific risks include: 1) unexpected changes in the underlying global

macroeconomic environment; 2) currency and interest rate movement; and 3) commodity
price inflation.

Investing in structured investments involves significant risks. For a detailed discussion of the risks involved in investing in any particular structured investment, you must read

the relevant offering materials for that investment. Structured investments are unsecured obligations of a particular issuer with returns linked to the performance of an
underlying asset. Depending on the terms of the investment, investors could lose all or a substantial portion of their investment based on the performance of the underlying
asset. Investors could also lose their entire investment if the issuer becomes insolvent. UBS does not guarantee in any way the obligations or the financial condition of any

issuer or the accuracy of any financial information provided by any issuer. Structured investments are not traditional investments and investing in a structured investment is
not equivalent to investing directly in the underlying asset. Structured investments may have limited or no liquidity, and investors should be prepared to hold their investment
to maturity. The return of structured investments may be limited by a maximum gain, participation rate or other feature. Structured investments may include call features and,

if a structured investment is called early, investors would not earn any further return and may not be able to reinvest in similar investments with similar terms. Structured
investments include costs and fees which are generally embedded in the price of the investment. The tax treatment of a structured investment may be complex and may differ
from a direct investment in the underlying asset. UBS and its employees do not provide tax advice. Investors should consult their own tax advisor about their own tax situation

before investing in any securities.

We see several indicators suggesting strong ABF equipment demand, including (1) the 
lead time for ABF vacuum laminators has extended into 2031, implying ~1.5k units of 
mid-to-high end equipment orders (assuming UTR is full for Eternal Precision’s capacity 
in both Japan and China throughout 2027-31), vs. ~600 units of total equipment 
shipped during 2022-25. This suggests demand for ABF equipment is strengthening 
(even stronger than previous upcycle in 2020-22) and shifting toward higher-end 
(management mentioned that the four major Taiwanese ABF customers mainly ordered 
the 90-ton 3-stage vacuum laminators for new capacity expansions), (2) Eternal 
Precision has seen some customers voluntarily offer to pay a premium to secure 
equipment supply and shorten the long lead time, a trend that was not observed by the 
company during the last upcycle, and (3) contract liabilities uptrend has just started 
(Exhibit 2), and we expect to see even stronger growth in coming years considering the 
upcoming ABF substrate industry capacity expansion trend (from 2027 to at least 2031, 
Exhibit 3) 
The extended order lead time for the key ABF equipment supports our positive view on 
the ABF substrate industry. ABF suppliers remain proactive in capacity expansion, but 
demand continues to outpace supply, thus we expect the shortage ratio of ABF 
substrates could reach 14%/34%/51% in 2H26/2027/2028 (see here). We see limited 
risk from capacity expansion by China ABF suppliers due to their limited access to new 
ABF equipment. Eternal Precision has seen some China ABF customers offering 
premiums to shorten equipment lead time, but the company mentioned that they 
prioritize major customers, as well as those with secured ABF film supply (we continue to 
see limitations on ABF film supply for China ABF suppliers). Meanwhile, more 
end-customers are asking key ABF suppliers for capacity beyond 2029, consistent with 
the fact that major ABF suppliers have better access to key ABF equipment. 
In terms of future substrate products, Unimicron previously mentioned that MP 
shipment of EMIB-T substrates could start in 2027 (see here). While Japanese ABF 
suppliers will be the first to have access to the new ABF vacuum laminators for EMIB-T 
substrates given their better production yields, Unimicron is tracking close by targeting a 
50% yield for this new product. For the more advanced glass core substrates, we 
continue to see Kinsus and Unimicron making progress with this new technology (see 
here and here), while commercialization is unlikely in the near term (beyond 2029 per 
GSe). Overall, we believe that the most advanced packaging substrates could become a 
key revenue/GM driver in the longer term given their better pricing/margin profile (most 
advanced packaging substrates typically have GM of 60%+ upon MP vs. 
Kinsus/Unimicron GM at 26.1%/24.8% as of 2Q26). 
Maintain our Buy ratings on NYPCB (on CL) and Kinsus, but stay Neutral on Unimicron, 
with unchanged 12m TPs for NYPCB/Kinsus/Unimicron of 
NT$2,500/NT$1,125/NT$1,220.  
Investment Thesis & PT methodology and risks

Investment Thesis - NYPCB 
NYPCB manufactures and markets printed circuit boards (PCBs) and integrated circuit 
(IC) substrates (including ABF and BT substrates). We are Buy-rated on NYPCB 
16 September 2026   3
Goldman Sachs Taiwan ABFFor the exclusive use of KEVINLU@LENOVO.COM
e92c7a75ab8b4efbba794e6b187208c8

### Page 4

considering the company’s high exposure to BT substrates and non-LTA ABF substrate
(70%+ of total revenue), where we expect GM/OPM to increase significantly in the
coming quarters (from breakeven in OPM in 1H25 to 27/41% in 2026/27E) due to the
favorable substrate pricing outlook. Despite the company’s low exposure to high-end
ABF substrate industry (<20% of total revenue in 1H25), we believe the expanding
demand from ASIC AI server and high-end switch IC demand will drive the company’s

high-end ABF revenue exposure to 40%+ by 2027E, suggesting better GM/OPM outlook
in the long term. In 2H25-2H26, we believe NYPCB will continue to benefit from stronger
ABF/BT pricing and should be a key beneficiary of the pricing uptrend considering the
company’s key focus on BT and non-LTA ABF substrate industries. Moreover, in the long
term (after 2026), we expect the ABF industry to return to supply tightness, and NYPCB’s
expanding high-end exposure will lead to a long-term earnings growth outlook (122%

2026-28E earnings CAGR). As a result, we see solid upside potential for NYPCB’s
earnings and stock performance.
Price Target Risks and Methodology - NYPCB
Valuation methodology: Our 12-month target price of NT$2,500 for NYPCB is based
on 21x 2028E P/E, which is +1.5x STDV higher than the past upcycle average P/E (13.3x).
Key downside risks: (1) Slower-than-expected PC demand recovery, (2)
Slower-than-expected ABF substrates pricing upgrading outlook, and (3)

Slower-than-expected NYPCB new high-end capacity qualification progress.
Investment Thesis - Kinsus
Kinsus is one of the key IC substrates suppliers globally with ~10% ABF/BT substrates
market share in 2024, with ~18% of total 2025 revenue contributed by its contact lens
subsidiary, Pegavision (6941.TW, Not Covered). With our view that the ABF substrate
shortage started from 2Q26 (ABF substrate return to supply demand balance from late
2025), we believe Kinsus’ performance will continue to improve compared with high-end

ABF peers considering the company’s increasing market share in the high-end ABF
substrate market; however, ~36% of the company’s revenue was contributed by BT
substrates in 2025 and 30% of the company’s ABF revenue is from non-LTA customers,
which we believe will continue to enjoy a better pricing outlook. As a result, we believe
Kinsus’ earnings outlook should continue to be strong (~106% 2026-29E CAGR), mainly
driven by better BT substrate and non-LTA ABF substrate pricing outlook, despite the

unfavorable demand outlook before 2H26. We believe Kinsus is an experienced
substrate supplier with a clear capacity expansion plan that can also upgrade its product
portfolio in the long term. We believe we are at the beginning of an industry upcycle,
making us positive on Kinsus’ stock performance over the next 12 months.
Price Target Risks and Methodology - Kinsus
Valuation methodology: Our 12-month target price of NT$1,125 for Kinsus is based on

16.5x 2028E P/E, which is +1.2x STDV higher than Kinsus’ past upcycle average P/E
(14.2x).
Key downside risks: (1) Slower-than-expected PC demand recovery, (2)
Slower-than-expected ABF substrates pricing upgrading outlook, (3)
Slower/Less-than-expected AI server orders from 2H26.

### Page 5

Investment Thesis - Unimicron Technology
Unimicron is a key supplier of ABF substrates (25% market share in 2025) and BT
substrates and also produces FPC/HDI/PCB products. We are Neutral rated on the
shares given long-term growth opportunity prospects: (1) over 70% of ABF shipments
are covered by long-term agreements (LTA), which limits pricing flexibility and makes
price hike less likely; (2) the company’s execution track record has been weaker than

peers; (3) capacity expansion plan remains slower than peers, constraining its ability to
capture incremental AI demand. The current ABF substrates market is seeing more
favorable supply demand outlook, and Unimicron is likely to see better pricing outlook
to reflect rising material costs in the coming quarters. AI ABF account for 10% of the
company’s total revenue, but the growth is likely to be slower than peers due to the
company continuing to lose market share to peers given its less favourable production

yield and slow capacity expansion. Overall, we are Neutral rated on Unimicron given our
outlook for limited upside potential, barring ASIC AI servers, despite our generally
positive view in the long term on its profitability trajectory.
Price Target Risks and Methodology - Unimicron Technology
Valuation methodology: Our 12-month target price of NT$1,220 for Unimicron is
based on 17.4x 2028E P/E, which is 2.7x STDV higher than the past upcycle average P/E

(10.3x).
Key upside/downside risks: (1) Slower/faster-than-expected PC demand recovery, (2)
Slower/faster-than-expected ABF substrates upgrading pace, and (3) Slower/faster
than-expected AI server PCB market share loss progress.

STOCK RATINGS
Morgan Stanley uses a relative rating system using terms such as Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated or Underweight (see definitions below). Morgan Stanley does not assign ratings of Buy,
Hold or Sell to the stocks we cover. Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated and Underweight are not the equivalent of buy, hold and sell.  Investors should carefully read the definitions of all
ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

## Document 125: 260918_daiwa_ Jentech

Source PDF: all report pdf/260918_daiwa_ Jentech.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-09-18
Pages: 8
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Taiwan Information Technology

What’s new: We received an update from Jentech on 18 September. Its 
share price has been negatively impacted week to date by market talk 
concerning the adoption of removable lids in Vera Rubin and Rubin Ultra. 
However, we maintain our positive view on its ongoing product-upgrade 
roadmap and expect the ASP-upgrade trend to continue in the following 
years. We reaffirm our Buy (1) call (see our latest memo, Record-high 
monthly revenue for August; 3Q26 QTD revenue tracking ahead, on 7 
September 2026).

What’s the impact: Promising ASP upgrade trend. Due to rising thermal 
design power (TDP), we expect the ASP of Jentech’s removable lid for 
Vera Rubin or Rubin Ultra to be 5-6x higher vs. that of the current one-
piece lid. Following better-than-expected shipments during the ramp-up of 
Vera Rubin, we now forecast its 3Q26 revenue at TWD9.65bn (previously 
TWD9.26bn vs. Bloomberg consensus of TWD9.62bn) and 4Q26 revenue 
at TWD10.62bn (previously TWD10.20bn, vs. Bloomberg consensus of 
TWD10.32bn). We believe there could be some revenue upside potential, 
subject to the adoption of removable lids for Vera Rubin or Rubin Ultra. We 
now forecast an EPS CAGR of 86.1% over 2026-28E, from 78.8% 
previously, as we believe further product upgrades and a favourable 
product mix should continue to support improving profitability.

Bright outlook for GPU projects as well as ASIC projects. We believe 
Jentech has a more promising product roadmap for ASIC clients, with 
potential ASP upgrades from the current stiffener. After GPU clients adopt 
removable lids, it also could encourage its ASIC clients to adopt removable 
lids, translating to 15-20x ASP increase from here. We raise our 2026-28E 
EPS by 6-15%, mainly as a result of our more aggressive revenue and 
gross-/operating-margin assumptions.

What we recommend: We reiterate our Buy (1) call and raise our 12M TP 
to TWD9,420 (from TWD6,770), still based on a target PER of 73x (vs. its 
past-3-year PER range of 17-62x) applied to our 1-year forward EPS 
forecast, which is rolled over to 4Q26E-3Q27E (from 3Q26E-2Q27E). We 
believe our target valuation is fair, given Jentech’s strong earnings visibility 
and leading position in chip-level thermal solutions, which we believe 
warrant a rerating in addition to its strong moat and competitive landscape 
in the segment. Downside risks: weaker-than-expected global AI server 
demand and slower-than-expected thermal spec upgrades.

How we differ: Our 2026-28E EPS are 1-10% above Bloomberg 
consensus, likely as we are more aggressive in our revenue and gross-
/operating-margin assumptions.

18 September 2026

3Q26 preview: stronger-than-expected spec upgrades

Company profile 
Established in 1987, Jentech Precision Industrial is a leading global thermal-solutions and precision-
metal components maker that serves leading semiconductor and server vendors. As of 2025, its 
revenue comprised thermal heat spreaders (73%), lead frames (9%), electronic components (7%), 
communication connectors (1%) and others (10%).

### Page 5

5

Jentech Precision Industrial (3653 TT): 18 September 2026 
ESG analysis

Update Date: 29 May 2026 
Source: Daiwa, Company

ESG risks
Risks Management Analyst comments
G
Executive/board
quality 1
The CEO and chairperson of Jentech is the same individual, Mr. Zhao Zongxin. We note that Mr.
Zhao has extensive long-term experience in senior management within the company and across
its subsidiaries, which is a positive sign from an executive perspective.
Capital management 1
Between 2021-25, Jentech has maintained a dividend payout ratio of around 60%, which we
consider reasonable for balancing shareholder returns with reinvestment needs. This consistent
approach reflects prudent capital management discipline and a stable financial policy.
Related party &
transaction 1
The company engages in transactions with subsidiaries primarily for normal operational
purposes, such as purchase of materials and components. The proportion of these costs to
overall COGS remains low, and no irregular or non-transparent transactions were identified.
E Supply chain
management 1
Jentech has a structured procurement process, including supplier evaluation, signature of
procurement contracts, and requirements for compliance with environmental and safety
regulations. The company also diversifies sourcing channels (major suppliers from Taiwan,
Japan, Europe, Korea, USA) and maintains stable supply relationships, with clear
communication protocols for prompt response to supplier issues. This reflects strong supply
chain transparency and robust quality control measures.
E Data security 1
The company has established an information security team, conducts annual internal control
audits, and implements procedures such as asset inventory, network security monitoring, virus
prevention systems, firewall management, and access rights control. Backup systems and
disaster recovery mechanisms are in place, allowing the company to restore information system
operations and minimize damage during major incidents or equipment failures.
Note: Management score represents a company's ability to manage/benefit from certain ESG topics. The scores range from 1 to 3, with 1 being the strongest.

1 群益投顧 
大立光 (3008 TT)  公告 斥資新台幣 11.72 億元購入台中市南屯區約 2,259.25 坪 土 地，以
及約 3,695.25 坪 建 物 ， 供 未 來 擴 充 產 能 使 用， 這 是 大 立 光 今 年 第 7 度購買不動產 ， 7
筆合計金額約 80.78 億 元，目 的 皆 為 未 來 擴 充 產 能 需 求，預 期 09/2026 建 立 試 產 線 後 將
可推進多個客戶 Fiber Array 訂 單，並 在 1H27 量產 ；展望後勢 ，iPhone 鏡 頭 出 貨 進 入
傳 統 旺 季 ， 預 期 2H26 營 運 呈 現 逐 季 成 長 趨 勢 ， 法 說 會 上 明 示FAU 零 組 件 進 度 ， 預 期
1H27 可 望 量 產 ， 顯 示 公 司 對 FAU 零 組 件 業 務 表 示 高 度 信 心 ， 有 助 於 提 升 本 益 比 ， 維
持 Buy 投資建議 。

大 立 光 今 年 累 計 7 次 出 手 購 地：  
大立光 (3008 TT) 公告 ，斥 資 新 台 幣 11.72 億 元 購 入 台 中 市 南 屯 區 約 2,259.25
坪 土 地 ， 以 及 約 3,695.25 坪 建 物 ， 供 未 來 擴 充 產 能 使 用 ， 這 是 大 立 光 今 年
第 7 度 購 買 不 動 產 ，7 筆 合 計 金 額 約 80.78 億 元 ， 目 的 皆 為 未 來 擴 充 產 能 需
求，預期 09/2026 建 立 試 產 線 後 將 可 推 進 多 個 客 戶 Fiber Array 訂 單 ， 並 在
1H27 量產 。  
展望 3Q26 ， 雖 然 iPhone18 標 準 版 手 機 鏡 頭 延 後 出 貨 ， 但 是 首 款 iPhone 摺
疊 機 開 賣 ， 以 及 可 變 光 圈 鏡 頭 較 傳 統7P 鏡 頭 漲 價 ， 皆 有 助 於 推 動 鏡 頭 旺 季
效 應 ， 預 估 3Q26 營收 178.89 億元， QoQ+30.92% ； 由 於 可 變 光 圈 鏡 頭 為 新
產 品 ， 又 因 增 加 多 個 檢 測 工 站 使 良 率 更 低 ， 毛 利 率 將 低 於2Q26 ， 預 估 稅 後
淨利 62.67 億 元 ， QoQ+34.19% ， EPS47.91 元 。  
展望 2026 年， Apple 摺 疊 機 發 售 可 望 減 緩 iPhone 銷 量 下 滑 的 幅 度 ， 力 拼 整
體 iPhone 銷 量 持 平 ；iPhone 半 導 體 零 組 件 多 為 自 製 ，iPhone 抵 抗 成 本 上 揚
能力優於 Android 手 機，故 能 依 照 原 定 計 畫 升 級 高 階 機 種 主 鏡 頭 為 可 變 光 圈
鏡頭，可變光圈鏡頭因為需要多段光圈皆能精準對焦，而增加檢測站數將
導 致 良 率 下 降，因 此 鏡 頭 價 格 較 傳 統 7P 鏡 頭 更 高，預 估 2026 年營收 653.47
億元， YoY+6.87% ， 稅 後 淨 利 238.33 億 元 ， YoY+12.02% ， EPS1 79.64 元。
未來 FAU 業 務 將 成 為 影 響 本 益 比 高 低 的 重 要 因 素。  
iPhone 鏡 頭 出 貨 進 入 傳 統 旺 季 ， 預 期2H26 營 運 呈 現 逐 季 成 長 趨 勢 ， 法 說 會
上明示 FAU 零 組 件 進 度 ， 預 期 1H27 可 望 量 產 ， 顯 示 公 司 對 FAU 零 組 件 業
務 表 示 高 度 信 心 ， 有 助 於 提 升 本 益 比 ， 維 持Buy 投 資 建 議 。

產業速報  
                                        2026 年 9 月 18 日

3 群益投顧 
  【 投 資 評 等 說 明 】 
  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15%≤ 首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間<35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy)  5%≤ 首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間<15%

中立 (Neutral)  
無 法 由 基 本 面 給 予 投 資 評 等 
預 期 近 期 股 價 將 處 於 盤 整 
建 議 降 低 持 股

【 投 資 評 等 調 整 機 制 】 
  投 資 評 等 係 「 首 次 」 給 予 特 定 個 股 投 資 評 等 時 ， 其 前 一 日 收 盤 價 相 較3 個
月目標價之潛在上漲空間計算而得。個股投資評等分為 四 個等級，定義如
上 。 爾 後 的 投 資 評 等係 依 循 「 首 次 評 等 」 ， 直 到 停 止 推 薦 。  
「 強 力 買 進 」、「 買 進 」及「 區 間 操 作 」均有 upside 目 標 價 。 差 別 僅 在 於 ，
首 次 評 等 時 潛 在 報 酬 率 不 同 。 「 中 立 」 則 無 目 標 價 。 
一旦我們給予特定個股「強力買進」 、 「買進」 或「區間操作」 之投資評
等，就是責任的開始，爾後將透過各式研究報告作定期性、持續性追蹤基
本 面 及 股 價 變 化 ， 直 到 停 止 推 薦 。 
停 止 推 薦 情 境 ： 
1.  達 目 標 價 。 
2.  雖 未 達 原 訂 目 標 價 ， 但 檢 視 基 本 面 、 訊 息 面 、 籌 碼 面 等 多 方 訊 息 ，
研 判 股 價 上 漲 空 間 已 然 有 限 ， 將 適 時 出 具 降 評 報 告 。 
3.  推 薦 後 股 價 不 漲 反 跌 ， 亦 將 出 具 降 評 報 告 。

【免責聲明】  
  本研究報告僅提供予特定人之客戶作為參考資料「非經同意不得轉載」。
我們並不確保此資訊的完整性與正確性，投資人應了解，報告中有關未來
預測之陳述可能不會實現，因而不應被依賴。而且此報告並非根據特定投
資 目 的 或 依 預 定 對 象 之 財 務 狀 況 所 撰 寫 出 來 的，因 此，此 研 究 報 告 的 目 的 ，
既非對投資人於買賣證券、選擇權、期貨或其他證券相關之衍生性商品提
供詢價服務，亦非作為進行交易的要約。投資人應注意到相關證券之價值
及收益，可能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投
資 來 得 少 的 情 形 。

本次報告：公司拜訪

雙鴻 (3324 TT) 
水冷營收貢獻比重放大

永豐觀點 
水冷散熱營收貢獻大，持續關注獲利率表現。 
 投資評價與建議 
研究部維持中立建議：2H26 受惠 GB300 系列水冷散熱相關產品出貨增加，以及
AWS Trainium3及輝達 Vera Rubin 世代相關水冷產品開始出貨，逐漸增添貢獻。
Trainium3 4Q26 主要為小量產階段，預期主要貢獻放量將落在 2027 年。雙鴻目
前正在驗證輝達 Vera Rubin 世代水冷板，預計最快 9 月底通過驗證，待驗證完成
後進一步加入營收獲利貢獻預估。 
隨著 AI 伺服器散熱需求越來越高，不論 GPU、ASIC 散熱設計都越趨複雜，也加
大投資需求，對獲利率表現形成挑戰。水冷散熱供應者眾，目前水冷散熱零組件市
場以奇鋐及 Cooler Master 產能較大及佔據主要份額。雙鴻營運主要能受惠整體
水冷散熱滲透率、市場產值提升，帶動營收規模擴大及獲利金額成長。 預估雙鴻
2026 年、2027 年水冷散熱營收比重 59%、68%，稅後 EPS 56.93 元、79.87 元。
研究部維持中立建議。

### Page 2

2

雙鴻為台灣散熱模組廠商，主要提供風扇、散熱片、熱導管等設計組合而成散熱工
具，裝置在筆記型電腦、桌上型電腦、伺服器及智慧型手機等產品。高速運算晶片
發熱量持續增加， 散熱管理重要性提升。 散熱管理技術過往以主被動混合式熱管理
方案(鰭片+風扇)為主 ， 近年主流設計為均熱板 、 熱導管及3DVC 的散熱解決方案 ，
伺服器應用客戶積極導入水冷式散熱解決方案，六大關鍵零組件包含 Cold Plate、
CDU(Cooling Distribution Units)、Rack、RDHx、Quick Connectors、Manifold。 
雙鴻生產基地位於泰國、中國廣州、昆山、重慶、合肥。台灣散熱模組廠主要競爭
對手為奇鋐、Cooler Master、台達電、超眾及力致等等。客戶包括國內外筆電品
牌大廠及台灣 ODM 代工廠。2Q26 產品組合：PC 17%，VGA 4%，Server 78%，
Others 1%。 
水冷營收比重持續提高：先前雙鴻公告 7 月自結獲利，單月營收 37.7 億元，稅前
淨利 8.3 億元，稅後淨利 6.0 億元，稅後 EPS 6.47 元，優於預期，主因 7 月認列
部份 6 月遞延營收。8 月份客戶拉貨波動，單月營收月減 16.6%，預期 9 月份將
會回升。3Q26 雙鴻 GB300 水冷板、Manifold 出貨增加，以及預期 9 月份 AWS 
Trainium3 及輝達 Vera Rubin 世代相關水冷產品開始出貨，逐漸增加貢獻，主要
貢獻將落在 4Q26。 
研究部推估雙鴻 3Q26、4Q26 水冷營收比重達 61％、63%。營收分別為 103 億、
109 億元。水冷營收比重增加，同時增強獲利率表現，預估毛利率分別為29.9%、
30.3%，稅後 EPS 16.77 元、17.14 元。 
認證輝達 Vera Rubin 水冷板中 ：雙鴻目前正在驗證輝達 Vera Rubin 世代水冷板 ，
預計最快 9 月底通過驗證。研究部認為短期雙鴻出貨 Vera Rubin 世代相關水冷產
品將先以 Manifold 為主，待驗證完成後進一步加入營收獲利貢獻預估。同時雙鴻
也有望延續進入 Vera Rubin Ultra 專案，待後續相關水冷產品開發。 
ASIC 方面，雙鴻將量產 AWS Trainium3 水冷相關產品包含水冷板、 Inner 
Manifold、Rack Manifold，惟 4Q26 主要為小量產階段，預期主要貢獻放量將落
在 2027 年。後續包含 Meta ASIC 及 CDU 產品都有望額外增添貢獻雙鴻水冷營
收。 
產能持續擴充 ， 主要受惠水冷市場產值擴大 ：公司積極拓展伺服器水冷散熱市場機
會，2026 年、2027 年持續增加伺服器水冷產品產能。目前水冷板月產能包含廣州
及泰國共 400k pcs， 後續視情況在泰國增加100k~200k pcs。Manifold 台灣 2k、
泰國 3k。隨著 AI 伺服器散熱需求越來越高，不論 GPU、ASIC 散熱設計都越趨複
雜，如無塵室等級要求提高，也加大投資需求，對獲利率表現都會形成挑戰。 
水冷散熱供應者眾，目前水冷散熱零組件市場以奇鋐及 Cooler Master 產能較大
及佔據主要份額。雙鴻營運主要能受惠整體水冷散熱滲透率、市場產值提升，帶動
營收規模擴大及獲利金額成長。預估雙鴻 2026 年、2027 年水冷散熱營收比重
59%、68%。2026 年營收 385 億元，年增 65%，毛利率 29.6%，營益率 17.8%，
稅後 EPS 56.93 元。2027 年營收 513 億元，年增33%，毛利率 18.6%，稅後 EPS 
79.87 元。

3Q26 營收及獲利表現強勁 
3Q26 進入旺季，板卡業務復甦，Server 料源轉好，遞延訂單開始出貨，估
營收季增 55%至 156億元，年增 34%。低毛利率 AI Server出貨較多，估毛
利率季減 3百分點至 17%，營業利益 15.3億元，季增 66%，年增 145%。
估稅後 7億元，季增 93%，年增 31%，EPS 5.7元，比原先預估上修 19%，
主因高毛利率一般型 Server 比重上升及 PC 相關產品(MB、VGA)業績谷底
翻揚，造成毛利率及本業獲利比原先預期佳。 
記憶體大漲，但客戶殺價情況減緩，反而有助整體毛利率上升 
公司終端產品漲價減低記憶體大漲影響，客戶殺價情況亦減緩，有助整體毛
利率上升，1Q/2Q26毛利率 15%/20%，遠比 2025年 13.6%佳。下半年 AI 
Server料源逐漸改善，占比上升，估華擎 3Q/4Q26降至 17.0%/15.8%，但
全年毛利率 16.7%，年增 3.1百分點。2027年受 AI Server GB系列開始出
貨，毛利率降至 15.5%，年減 1.2百分點，但營收規模擴大，費用率下滑，
估 2026/27年營業利益率 8.9%/8.3%，比 2025年 5.7%佳。 
2026/27 年營收及獲利維持 20%以上高成長 
2026/27年受惠資料中心建置加快，企業端推論需求提高，估一般型 Server
營收成長 73%/18%。2026/27 年北美、亞洲等 新客戶訂單增加， 估 AI 
Server 營收成長 57%/43%(占整體營收比重 51.1%/56.7%)。合計 2026/27
年整體 Server營收(一般型+AI)年增 61%/37%，占比 70%/74%。 
合計華擎 2026/27年營收年增 20%/29%至 574/743億元(2026年下修 4%，
主因 AI Server 缺料)，估 2026/27 年毛利率 16.7%/15.5%，比 2025 年
13.55%改善。合計稅後 23.5/29.5億元，年增 24%/26%，EPS 1 8.98/23.87
元，比先前預估略下修 1%/1%，主因少數股權項目扣除變多 。受惠 AI 
Server需求高，工業電腦明顯復甦，華擎獲利表現仍佳，維持買進評等。 
元大觀點 
⧫  
3Q26 進入旺季，板卡業務復甦， Server 料源轉好，遞延訂單開始出
貨，估營收季增 55%至 156 億元，年增 34%。 
⧫  
記憶體大漲， 但客戶殺價情況減緩， 反而 有助整體毛利率上升 ，估
2026/27 年毛利率 16.7%/15.5%，比 2025 年 13.6%上升。 
⧫  
AI Server 及 IPC 需求高，毛利率上升，獲利表現佳，維持買進評等。
目標價 280 元係以本益比 12 倍、2H26~1H27 EPS 23.55 元推得。

營運分析
3Q26 營收及獲利表現強勁
2Q26受 Server的 CPU缺貨影響，營收季減 24%至 101億元，年減 16%，但 AI Server 訂單仍強
勁。3Q26 進入旺季，板卡業務復甦， Server 料源轉好，遞延訂單開始出貨，估營收季增 55%至
156億元，年增 34%。低毛利率 AI Server 出貨較多，估毛利率季減 3百分點至 17%，營業利益
15.3億元，季增 66%，年增 145%。業外在利息收入及其他收入挹注下，估獲利 0.4億元。合計稅
後 7 億元，季增 93%，年增 31%，EPS 5.7 元，比原先預估上修 19%，主因高毛利率一般型
Server 比重上升及 PC 相關產品(MB、VGA)業績谷底翻揚，造成毛利率及本業獲利比原先預期佳。

4Q26 進入最旺季，板卡業務持續復甦，Server 需求暢旺，估營收季增 17%，年

4Q26 進入最旺季，板卡業務持續復甦， Server 需求暢旺，估營收季增 17%至 183 億元，年增
34%。低毛利率 AI Server  GB開始出貨，估毛利率季減 1.2百分點至 15.8%，營業利益 16.9億元，
季增 11%，年增 120%。業外在利息收入及其他收入挹注下，估獲利 0.4億元。合計稅後 7.64億
元，季增 8%，年增 54%，EPS 6.19元，獲利穩定向上。

記憶體短缺且價格大漲，客戶殺價情況亦減緩，反而有助整體毛利率上升 
1Q26記憶體大漲，華擎與客戶合作因應，VGA/Server相關產品向客戶動態報價，客戶自行採買更
便宜記憶體或減少記憶體配置量，降低影響； IPC 則因記憶體配備無法輕易變動，較難如 Server
採減少配置作法，對價格敏感的客戶會遞延拉貨。需求部分，MB 出貨雖未配備 DRAM，但終端產
品漲價，消費性電子客戶較難適應，出貨下滑；其餘產品銷售亦受波及，但比 MB影響略小。 
公司終端產品漲價減低記憶體大漲影響， 且記憶體、 CPU、GPU 料源不足，造成低毛利率 AI 
Server出貨不順，客戶殺價情況亦減緩，反而有助整體毛利率上升，1Q/2Q26毛利率 15%/20%，
遠比 2025 年 13.6%佳。下半年 AI Server 料源逐漸改善，占比上升，因此估華擎 3Q/4Q26 降至
17.0%/15.8%，但全年毛利率 16.7%，年增 3.1百分點。2027年受 AI Server GB系列開始出貨，
毛利率降至 15.5%，年減 1.2 百分點，但營收規模擴大，費用率下滑，估 2026/27 年營業利益率
8.9%/8.3%，遠比 2025年 5.7%佳。

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：主機板 2026年 9月 17日 ASRock 第4 頁，共 17頁  
資料中心建置加速，Server 銷售樂觀 
2026/27 年受惠資料中心建置加快，企業端推論需求提高，一般型 Server 出貨暢旺，估營收成長
73%/18%。AI Server方面，2026/27年北美、亞洲等新客戶訂單增加，估 AI Server營收持續成長
57%/43%(占整體營收比重 51.1%/56.7%)。AI Server 一台單價高達 30~40萬美金，但大部分關鍵
零組件如： GPU、CPU、DRAM 等均外購，毛利率偏低，對毛利率走勢略為不利影響。 合計
2026/27年整體 Server營收(一般型+AI)年增 61%/37%，占比 70%/74%。 
圖 5：華擎 Server 營收成長顯著

資料來源：元大投顧預估 
2026/27 年營收及獲利維持 20%以上高成長 
1)工業電腦產業龍頭研華 B/B ratio 下半年維持在 1.4~1.5高檔，景氣復甦確立，邊緣 AI成為趨勢，
2025年成長 30%，2026/27年受惠一些專案陸續出貨(主要增加北美串流服務商大單)，成長加快至
81%/18%。 
2)MB/VGA從 2025年下半年記憶體大漲，銷售開始受影響，估 2026年營收分別衰退 39%/30%，
2027年上游原物料漲價趨緩，加上 AMD及 Intel有新品推出，估成長 7%/14%。 
合計華擎 2026/27年預估營收年增 20%/29%至 574/743億元(2026下修 4%，主因 AI Server缺料)，
記憶體大漲，客戶殺價情況亦減緩，反而有助整體毛利率上升 ，估華擎 2026/27 年毛利率
16.7%/15.5%，比 2025 年 13.55%改善。合計稅後 23.5/29.5 億元，年增 24%/26%，EPS 
18.98/23.87元，比先前預估略下修 1%/1%，主因少數股權項目扣除變多。

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：主機板 2026年 9月 17日 ASRock 第6 頁，共 17頁  
產業概況  
2026 年 PC 出貨恐衰退 6.3% 
受到上游記憶體成本上揚，PC廠商即使轉嫁或降低規格，均將影響消費者購買意願，因此將 2026
年 PC (NB+DT)出貨量下調至衰退 6.3%，NB亦下修至衰退 5.8%，產品項目中電競 NB成長 1.4%，
仍維持成長(其餘產品均衰退)，主因電競 NB具剛性需求，目前在 NB滲透率 15%仍低。華擎在電
競產品含：主機板、繪圖卡等，比重約 25%，在 PC不景氣時，銷售具有支撐。

### Page 7

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：主機板 2026年 9月 17日 ASRock 第7 頁，共 17頁  
圖 9：全球電競 NB 出貨量預估

資料來源：IDC、元大投顧預估 
AI、ASIC、通用型強勢出擊，2026 年美系 CSP 資本支出估年增 91%  
在 ChatGPT 等 AI 應用陸續推向市場，各大業者紛紛開始研發自有超級大模型，包括 Gemini 
(Google)、Claude (Anthropic)、Llama (Meta) 等，各大品牌開始大舉投入大型語言模型(LLM)市場，
且每一個產品的智慧皆持續提高，部分消費者也陸續從 ChatGPT 陣營轉移至 Gemini 陣營，反映
出消費者開始逐漸提升對 AI的黏著度。根據 Grand View Research 調查，2024 年全球 AI 市場營
收預估約 2,792 億美元，預估 2030 年市場規模超過 3 兆美元，擁有超過 30% 的年複合增長率。 
面對這爆發性的長期成長趨勢，CSP業者(Amazon、Microsoft、Google、Meta)在法說會上一致強
調，AI投資已轉變為一項橫跨 2026 年的結構性戰略，CSP大廠不僅持續擴大對 GB300等次世代
AI 伺服器的採購，更將重點放在資料中心基礎設施的長期戰略規劃，以應對 AI 運算對電力及散熱
系統強勁成長的需求，確保未來數年內擁有足夠的能源與容量來支持持續迭代的大模型部署。本中
心認為，2026年美國四大 CSP資本支出將年增 91%，且將有一半的成長來自於資料中心建置，代
表了資料中心的不足及未來伺服器需求。明年不僅僅是 NVIDIA GB300 的天下，AWS、Google、
Meta ASIC的崛起也將是一大關鍵，其中又以 Google TPU需求最為強勁，已經有兩個客戶預計將
採購 TPU。 
另外，受惠於 AI訓練與推論需求爆發的連帶效應，本中心預估 2026年通用型伺服器出貨量將年增
23%，其中以存儲型伺服器需求最為強勁。隨著生成式 AI 應用普及和使用者數量暴增，CSP 等業
者必須不斷擴充容量，且不僅限於高階 GPU 伺服器，龐大的 AI模型訓練和推論在使用狀態中將消
耗大量的數據(DATA)，使得存儲型伺服器的需求大增。

### Page 8

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：主機板 2026年 9月 17日 ASRock 第8 頁，共 17頁

下修 2026年營收 4%，主因 AI Server缺料，2027年營收維持不變。華擎 2026/27年預估營收年
增 20%/29%至 574/743 億元，記憶體大漲，客戶殺價情況亦減緩，反而有助整體毛利率上升，估
華擎 2026/27年毛利率 16.7%/15.5%，比 2025年 13.55%改善。合計稅後 23.5/29.5億元，年增
24%/26%，EPS 18.98/23.87元，比先前預估略下修 1%/1%，主因少數股權項目扣除變多。

改善，下半年營收成長加快，維持買進評等。目標本益比由先前 14倍下修至 12倍，主因記憶體大
漲，本業 PC相關業務復甦較慢，以 2H26~1H27EPS 23.55元計算，目標價 280元，潛在上漲空
間 32.7%。

### Page 13

公司簡介  
華擎成立於 2002年 5月，原為華碩子公司，以銷售中、低階主機板為主，後跨入工業電腦、伺服器等領域。後來華碩、和
碩分割後，華擎成為和碩子公司。產品組合(2025年)：主機板 20%、工業電腦 4%、一般伺服器 13%、AI伺服器 39%、其
他 24%。旗下設二家子公司，分別為永擎(持股 60%；主攻伺服器)、東擎(持股 64%；主打工業電腦)。華擎未設工廠，產
品主要交由和碩及陸商(中國、越南設廠)代工。公司未來發展以非 PC產品為主，有助分散營運風險。

### Page 14

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：主機板 2026年 9月 17日 ASRock 第14 頁，共 17頁

ESG 分析  
分別藉由「在 ESG議題上的曝險」和「個別公司在 ESG議題上的執行力」兩個面向來衡量公司於 ESG上的表現。 
► ESG 總分： 華擎整體的 ESG風險評級屬於中風險，於 Sustainalytics資料庫所覆蓋的公司中排名位於中間位置，在科
技硬體行業的公司中排名則略為落後同業。 
► 在 ESG 議題上的曝險： 華擎的整體曝險屬於低水準，且略優於科技硬體行業的平均水準。公司較需要改善的  ESG 議
題包含商業道德、人力資源和公司的數據安全等。 
► 個別公司在 ESG 議題上的執行力：華擎在針對重大 ESG議題的管理水準和執行力屬中等，已採取若干措施來管理與實
質 ESG議題相關的風險，公司擁有完整對 ESG的揭露，包含成立永續發展辦公室及發布 CSR報告書等，展現了對企
業社會責任的實踐。但在關鍵領域如職場多元性、供應鏈管理仍缺乏相應的政策與計畫。儘管如此，該公司並未捲入具
有重大財務影響的風險事件。

投資評等說明 
買進：根據本中心對該檔個股投資期間絕對或相對報酬率之預測，我們對該股持正面觀點。此一觀點係基於本中心對該股之發展前景、財務表現、利多題
材、評價資訊以及風險概況之分析。建議投資人於投資部位中增持該股。 
持有-超越同業：本中心認為根據目前股價，該檔個股基本面吸引力高於同業。此一觀點係基於本中心對該股發展前景、財務表現、利多題材、評價資訊
以及風險概況之分析。 
持有-落後同業：本中心認為根據目前股價，該檔個股基本面吸引力低於同業。此一觀點係基於本中心對該股發展前景、財務表現、利多題材、評價資訊
以及風險概況之分析。 
賣出：根據本中心對該檔個股投資期間絕對或相對報酬率之預測，我們對該股持負面觀點。此一觀點係基於本中心對該股之發展前景、財務表現、利多題
材、評價資訊以及風險概況之分析。建議投資人於投資部位中減持該股。 
評估中：本中心之預估、評等、目標價尚在評估中，但仍積極追蹤該個股。 
限制評等：為遵循相關法令規章及/或元大之政策，暫不給予評等及目標價。

天鈺(4961.TT) 
產業：電子–半導體 
下半年逆風成⻑，營運優於上半年  
 投資評價與建議 
考量公司下半年營收仍逆風成⻑，營運優於上半年，且成功在部分產品進行漲
價來轉嫁成本，毛利率有望回升，給予PER 16倍(以2027年EPS評價)。 
 重點評論分析 
預計3Q26營運成⻑動能延續，推升單季營收季增9% ：預計3Q26營收季增
8.5%，主要受惠市佔率擴大與全產品線新品量產，帶動8月單月營收達21.1億
元創三年來新高，3Q26 Mobile出貨將微幅下滑，佔比下降至32~37%，且最
競爭的TDDI毛利率已回升至健康水準 ， 非驅動IC產品線將會回升 ， 有望帶動毛
利率上升至27.4%，稅後EPS約3.09元。 
終端疲弱添變數，但下半年營收仍逆風成⻑、優於上半年：(1)雖然終端疲弱添
變數，但市佔率擴大與新品量產帶動下半年營收優於上半年，全新的手機
A M O L E D新產品預計於3 Q 2 6驗證導入(目前O L E D營收佔比約1 ~ 2 % )，
AMOLED筆電驅動IC預估4Q26量產，新⼀代EPD PMIC成功導入北美客戶機
種出貨 ， 2H26持續量產出貨 ， ESL開發E6多彩規格與GOA面板技術應用 ， Edge 
AI晶片訂單在智慧家居與生物識別領域放量，4Q26出貨量呈現翻倍成⻑
( Edge AI營收佔比約1%)。(2)自1Q26起，晶圓代工廠與封測廠等上游供應鏈
每季持續對客戶漲價，公司已成功在部分產品調漲以轉嫁成本，2H26整體毛
利率具備進步與回升的空間。 
 投資風險 
終端消費電子疲弱、晶圓代工廠與封測廠漲價導致成本上升。

| 營運概況  
Mobile IC 與大中尺寸顯示器驅動 IC 帶動 2Q26 營收季增 19.7%，預計 3Q26 營運動能延續推升營收季增 9%：
2Q26 營收 53.9 億元，營收季增 19.7%，主要係因 Mobile IC、大/中尺寸顯示器驅動 IC 與電源管理 IC 業務皆有
所成⻑，抵銷了 Other Semi 微幅下滑的波動，Mobile IC 第⼆季成⻑最為顯著，主要受惠手機、TFT/OLED IoT
產品與平板 TDDI 大量出貨，大/中尺寸顯示器驅動 IC 方面，則受惠公司打入新客戶，新產品量產與價格上揚帶動
整體營收提升(季成⻑動能依序為 Auto>Monitor>TV)，毛利率 26.9%，季減 0.9 個百分點，主要受生產成本上升
及毛利率最低的 Mobile 產品線出貨大幅增加，營收佔比上升至 38.6%，營業利益率 9.2%，季增 2.7 個百分點，
主要係因營業費用嚴格控管，單季歸屬⺟公司業主淨利為 3.84 億元，單季稅後 EPS 3.21 元。 
預計 3Q26 營運成⻑動能延續，推升單季營收季增 8.5%，受惠市佔率擴大與全產品線新品量產帶動，8 月單月營
收達 21.1 億元創三年來新高，3Q26 Mobile 佔比有望下降至 32~37%，且最競爭的 TDDI 毛利率已回升至健康
水準，預計帶動毛利率上升至 27.4%，稅後 EPS 約 3.09 元。 
終端疲弱添變數，下半年營收仍逆風成⻑、優於上半年：(1)市佔率擴大與新品量產帶動下半年營收優於上半年，
Mobile IC 方面，推出⼀系列 TDDI 新產品，目前正在客戶端快速驗證中，全新的手機 AMOLED 新產品亦預計
於 3Q26 驗證導入，AMOLED 筆電驅動 IC 預估 4Q26 量產，此外，受惠於燃油車與電動車內建螢幕數量持續增
加，車用驅動 IC 成⻑顯著，3Q26 佔比已升至高個位數百分比，成為中⻑期穩定的營運驅動引擎。手機相關 PMIC
於印度、中國擴張市佔帶動絕對⾦額上升，新⼀代 EPD PMIC 成功導入北美客戶機種出貨，2H26 持續量產出貨，
ASIC PMIC 預估 4Q26 完成工程樣品並於年底導入客製專案。其他相關半導體產品線方面，ESL 開發 E6 多彩規
格與 GOA 面板技術應用，T-Con 具備獨家色彩強化演算法，可節省外部 DRAM 成本，全新規格預估 4Q26 量
產，Edge AI 晶片訂單在智慧家居與生物識別領域放量，預估 4Q26 出貨量較 3Q26 呈現翻倍成⻑。(2)布局機器
人、無人機等新興科技領域，搶攻⻑期結構性成⻑趨勢。公司積極開發不同應用級別的邊緣運算，針對協作機器
人與機械手臂防護、Power 及 MCU 相關應用進行開發，無人機主打提供高性價比、平價的邊緣 AI 晶片，提供
2D/3D 視覺辨識的方案，亦參與經濟部關於機器人、無人機與低軌衛星的自主研發計劃。(3)雖然面臨晶圓代工與
封測漲價導致毛利率仍承壓，但積極與客戶溝通轉嫁成本：自 1Q26 起，晶圓代工廠與封測廠等上游供應鏈每季
持續對客戶漲價，公司已成功在部分產品調漲以轉嫁成本，2H26 整體毛利率具備進步與回升的空間。

### Page 3

3
| 公 司 簡 介
天鈺(4961)是顯示驅動 IC 與電源管理 IC 設計廠商 ， 產品線涵蓋大/中尺寸顯示器驅動 IC(Display IC) 、 中小尺寸
驅動 IC (Mobile IC)、電源管理 IC(Power IC)及時序控制與電子紙驅動等其他相關半導體(Other Semi)解決方案，
在電子紙驅動 IC 及電子貨架標籤(ESL)市占率全球第⼀。2Q26 產品組合為 Other Semi 26.81%、Mobile IC 38.64%、
Display IC 24.50%、Power IC 10.02%。主要客戶包含面板廠友達、京東方、華星光電、元太，主要競爭對手包
括聯詠、奇景、瑞鼎、敦泰、矽創。
天鈺的其他相關半導體(Other Semi)產品線整合了電子貨架標籤(ESL)、電子紙顯示器(EPD)驅動、Edge AI 晶片與
時序控制晶片(T-Con)技術，ESL 跟 EPD 廣泛應用於零售電子標籤跟電子紙顯示器，市占率約 80~90%為全球第
⼀， Edge AI 晶片應用於智慧家電、生物識別與 AI 玩具的控制，滿足邊緣運算的部署需求。大尺寸 LCD 顯示器

驅動 IC 主要應用於電視、桌上型顯示器及筆記型電腦等大尺寸面板，中尺寸用於車載與工控，天鈺在大/中尺寸
LCD 顯示器驅動 IC 市占率約 5~8%。小尺寸驅動 IC 廣泛應用於智慧型手機、平板電腦及穿戴式裝置，由於該市
場面臨嚴峻的價格壓力，其毛利率通常低於公司平均水準，公司產品線毛利率排序: Other Semi>大尺寸>公司平
均毛利率>小尺寸。為提升競爭門檻與獲利能力，天鈺積極進行產品線優化，PMIC 持續向非消費性、車用、工控
等高附加價值領域轉型，並積極開拓新產品與新市場，現有產品已擴張至印度、巴⻄等新興市場，應用層面涵蓋
直流無刷馬達、網通設備與智慧家電，此外，受惠於網通與家電轉向 Type-C 快充趨勢，其 PD 協議晶片出貨持續
增⻑，與客戶合作開發 ASIC PMIC 專案，預估於 4Q26 完成工程樣品，⻑線上規劃切入機器人、伺服器電源等高
毛利率市場，目前 Edge AI 佔比約 1%，OLED 營收佔比約 1~2%，未來將持續推升這兩大⻑線轉型產品的比例。

2026/09/17 投資速報

振曜 (6143.TT) 
長線彩色機種持續滲透，然短期仍難敵成本上漲

營收走勢圖

研究員/張書明 brady.chang@uni-psg.com

振曜主要生產電子閱讀器模組 ，研究部給予振曜中立投資建議 ： （1）
終端售價上漲，2Q26 財報不如預期，3Q26 營收達成率不如預期；
（2） 客戶下單保守，2H26 出貨量恐面臨衰退；（3）營收受成本
轉嫁膨脹使得毛利率下降，不過光纖與大尺寸等高毛利產品有望抵
銷部分影響。綜上所述，預估 26 年營收 106.5 億元下修至 91.7 
億元（+8%YoY）。 受惠子公司光纖產品維持強勁成長 ，毛利率從 21.0% 
微幅上修至 21.2%（-1.1ppts YoY），稅後 EPS 從 7.5 元下修至 
6.2 元。27 年彩色新機種問世後有望達雙位數營收佔比 ， 大尺寸與
光纖產品持續成長帶動產品組合轉佳，加上 26 年累積的換機潮，
預期 27 年營收 113 億元（+24%YoY），毛利率維持 20.9% 水準，
稅後 EPS 達 7.6 元重返成長。研究部考量終端漲價恐帶來不確定
性，目前評價尚屬合理，待後續觀察終端市場反應再行佈局。

重點摘要: 
1. 2Q26 財報不如預期，恐延續至 3Q26 
公司 2Q26 營收 23.1 億元（+15%QoQ，+12%YoY） ，不如原先
預期 24.8 億元，主要受終端需求不確定性、零組件成本上漲
與部分第一季提前拉貨影響。毛利率與費用率分別為  20.6% 
與 10.1%，略符合原先預期。2Q26 稅後 EPS 為 1.62 元，符
合預期。 
公司 8 月營收 6.7 億元（-7%MoM，-13%YoY），3Q26 營收達
成率 61%，低於預期。2H26 將受終端售價上漲導致消費環境
不佳，客戶恐下修訂單使得 2H26 旺季不旺。不過公司預期產
品單價將逐漸反應成本上漲 ， 未來營收將受到膨脹抵銷部分消
費環境帶來的衰退，預期  3Q26 營收從 29.1 億元下修至  
22.8 億元（-2%QoQ，-1%YoY），毛利率有望透過子公司沛亨
光纖業務佔比提升，從原先預估 20.1% 上修至 20.9%，預期 
3Q26 稅後 EPS 從 2.02 元下修至 1.36 元。

### Page 2

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  2

2. 儘管面臨供需雙重夾擊，彩色閱讀器仍逆勢成長 
目前大客戶 Kobo 彩色閱讀器營收佔比已達 70%，優於彩色
滲透率的 40%，顯示 Kobo 較高利基的彩色閱讀器更受歡迎，
隨著客戶在彩色機種的市占率持續提升 ， 振曜將一同受惠彩色
換機潮，2Q26 彩色佔比已逾 70%，帶動營收成長與產品組合
轉佳。1H26 出貨量較 1H25 仍維持成長個位數。 
然而近兩年因 AI 高速擴張使得記憶體需求劇增 ， 傳統記憶體
產能受到擠壓，DDR4 面臨嚴重供不應求而急速上漲逾十倍。
記憶體佔閱讀器 BOM cost 佔比已從 10% 成長至 30-40%。
而目前記憶體斷貨問題遠比漲價嚴重 ， 而公司和供應商合作多
年關係良好，去年底已經和供應商預先綁料，因此出貨無虞，
品牌商也將接受成本轉嫁，不過終究難免影響終端售價。6M26 
Kobo 宣布調整閱讀器產品售價約 +12-14%，8M26 領導品牌 
Kindle 亦宣布調漲 +12-14%。儘管調整後售價仍優於龍頭  
Kindle，研究部認為後續可能持續滾動式調漲。依據調整後消
費市場的態度，客戶對於 4Q26 訂單也更為保守看待。25 年 
Kobo 出貨量達兩百萬台 （+30%YoY），研究部保守預期26年
閱讀器出貨量從持平下修至衰退雙位數。 
新產品受到記憶體影響而有部分推遲， 預期從 4Q26 遞延至 
27 年問世，27 年新產品營收佔比有望突破雙位數，持續提升
產品競爭力。針對大尺寸產品，2025 年大尺寸廣告看板已出
貨 2000-3000 台，同樣受到記憶體與消費環境影響，加上供
應商大尺寸材料產能進度有所遞延，原預期 2026 年目標出貨
破萬台，將下修至 7000-9000 台，年增近四倍。透過彩色機
種、新機與廣告看板的佔比提升，產品組合持續轉佳有望降低
記憶體對於毛利的影響。

3. 光纖產業成長明確，子公司沛亨 26 年底擴廠年增五倍 
受惠  AI 光 纖 需 求 強 勁 成 長 ，子 公 司 沛 亨的 光 纖 客 戶 
Forecast 已到 27 年底，交期已拉長至 8-10 個月。目前最
大風險來自上游光纖絲材料 ， 不過公司長年與上游光纖廠合作
多年關係良好，目前已再次備足庫存，到 27 年底均供貨無
虞。針對上游光纖絲漲價的部分，公司認為報價已適度轉嫁，
預期隨著低價庫存見底 ， 公司毛利將成長至 26 年底逐步進入
高原期。面對供需持續吃緊，未來成長來自公司擴產速度。目
前公司維持兩班制， 產能已回至 23 年高峰水準 ， 預期到 4Q26 
有望再成長六七成，不過仍僅滿足需求 3-4 成。

### Page 3

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  3

公司同時積極找地建廠，預期最快可在 27 年底投產，產能規
模可望再翻倍，成長趨勢陡峭且明確。沛亨在振曜營收佔比將
從 26 年起突破三成，振曜 AI 光纖營收佔比也將因此突破
雙位數。

4. 長線彩色機種持續滲透，然短期仍難敵成本上漲 
儘管最大客戶 Kobo 為了確保出貨無虞而接受振曜成本轉嫁，
最終仍不敵成本影響而調整終端售價 ， 客戶對於後續訂單也更
為保守看待 ， 研究部保守預期 26 年閱讀器出貨量從持平下修
至衰退雙位數 ， 不過 26 年彩色機種營收佔比將突破七成持續
成長；大尺寸目標出貨量受到上游材料產能進度而有所遞延；
子公司維持強勁成長抵銷消費性產品帶來的營收與成本壓力，
也將使得非閱讀器佔比從三成提升至四成 。 產品組合改善對於
獲利仍具有保護效果。 
綜上所述，預估 26 年營收 106.5 億元下修至 91.7 億元
（+8%YoY）。受惠子公司光纖產品維持強勁成長， 毛利率從 
21.0% 微幅上修至 21.2%（-1.1ppts YoY） ， 稅後 EPS 從 7.5 
元下修至 6.2 元。27 年彩色新機種問世後有望達雙位數營
收佔比，大尺寸與光纖產品持續成長帶動產品組合轉佳，加上 
26 年累積的換機潮，預期 27 年營收 113 億元 （+24%YoY），
毛利率維持 20.9% 水準，稅後 EPS 達 7.6 元重返成長。

3Q26 GPU/ASIC 測試板如預期出貨，EPS 預計季增 11% 
預估 3Q26 營收季增 18%，主因 AI GPU 後段測試板及 ASIC 前段測試板進
入放量期。毛利率季減 2.6ppts 至 54.9%，主因新產品預燒測試板/SLT 測試
板將逐漸提高營收貢獻。EPS 季增 11.3%/年增 98.9%達 17 元。4Q26 營收
則受惠公司 8M26 第二波的產能擴張將開始用於出貨予預燒爐客戶，同時
TPU 新產品亦將進入量產期，預計營收季增 12%。毛利率年減 2.0 個百分
點至 54.0%，主因新產品預燒測試板 /SLT 測試板將逐漸提高營收貢獻使整
體產品組合轉差。然在營運規模擴大，預計 EPS 仍將季增 13%達 18.9 元。 
EV AI 探針卡通過驗證，產能提升下有望切入其餘專案 
目前公司已通過 EV AI 探針卡客戶驗證，預計在 1H27 將開始出貨量產探針
卡予客戶。此案件將採雙晶片(dual-die)的探針卡設計架構，單張探針卡針數
將超過 40000 針且預計在 2027 年將出貨超過 100 張探針卡。此外公司將利
用自製植針機及探針的優勢，將現有每月 7-80 萬 MEMS 探針產能於 2Q27
擴充至每月 120-130 萬根針，並切入該客戶採相似設計的其餘 AI CPU 探針
卡案件。目前該客戶 CPU 案件仍在工程驗證階段，預計於 4Q26-1Q27 tape 
out，CPU 探針卡量產營收貢獻則主要將落在 2H27。預計 2027 年受惠兩大
案件貢獻下，公司 HPC 探針卡營收將年增 282%，營收佔比由 8%提升到
17%。 
探針產能擴充及通過驗證有助於切入其餘案件並帶動評價提升
預計公司在 2027 年將出貨約 100 張 EV AI 探針卡，佔營收約 8%，成為公
司最大的單一探針卡專案。在公司預計將 MEMS 探針產能擴充 67%且雙晶
片探針卡技術已通過客戶認證之下，預計公司將有更多機會切入該客戶 採相
似設計其餘 AI CPU 探針卡案件。在探針卡有望切入客戶其餘 AI CPU 探針
卡案件且 HPC 測試板業務成長趨勢不變下，基於切入 AI 供應鏈後約 1x 標
準差的本益比 35x 與 2027F EPS NT$120 ，給予目標價 4250 元的買進評等。 
元大觀點 
⧫  
3Q26 EPS 季增 11%，主因 GPU/ASIC 測試板持續出貨，費用控制得
宜。4Q26 EPS 季增 13%主要受惠擴廠營收開始貢獻。 
⧫  
公司已通過 EV AI 探針卡客戶驗證，預計 2027 年將出貨超過 100 張探
針卡，佔營收約 8%，成為公司最大的單一探針卡專案。 
⧫  
探針產能提升 67%且通過技術驗證有助於公司切入其餘案件並帶動評
價提升。基於 35 倍 2027 年 EPS 120 元，給予 4250 元的買進評等。

營運分析
3Q26 GPU/ASIC 測試板如預期出貨，EPS 預計季增 11%
精測 7-8 月累積營收 12.5 億元，預估 3Q26 營收季增 18%/年增 56%至 19.4 億元，呈季年增主因
AI GPU 後段測試板及 ASIC 前段測試板進入放量期。毛利率季減 2.6/年減 1.4 個百分點至 54.9%，
主因新產品預燒測試板 /SLT 測試板將逐漸提高營收貢獻，然旗艦手機探針卡進入放量期將使毛利
率維持在公司毛利率指引 50-55%上緣。預計營業利益率季減 0.9/年增 9.1 個百分點至 33.8%，
EPS 季增 11.3%/年增 98.9%達 17 元。

4Q26 EPS 在擴廠產能使營運槓桿放大下將季增 13%
預估 4Q26 營收季增 12%/年增 81%至 21.7 億元，主因公司 8M26 第二波的產能擴張將開始用於出
貨予預燒爐客戶，同時 TPU 新產品亦將進入量產期，亦將帶動相關後段測試板的持續拉貨。毛利
率季減 0.9/年減 2.0 個百分點至 54.0%，主因新產品預燒測試板/SLT 測試板將逐漸提高營收貢獻使
整體產品組合轉差且高毛利 HPC 探針卡量產營收將主要落在 1H27。然在營運規模擴大且公司維持
過往費用控制的政策下，預計營業利益率將季增 0.7/年增 7.8 個百分點至 34.5%，EPS 預計季增
13%/年增 118%達 18.9 元。

### Page 3

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：半導體 2026 年 9 月 10 日 Chunghwa Precision Test 第3 頁，共 13頁

EV AI 探針卡通過客戶驗證，在產能提升下有望切入同一客戶其餘專案 
目前公司已通過 EV AI 探針卡客戶驗證，預計在 1H27 將開始出貨量產探針卡予客戶。此案件將採
雙晶片的探針卡設計架構，單張探針卡針數將超過 40000 針且預計在 2027 年將出貨約 100 張探針
卡，佔營收約 8%，成為公司最大的單一探針卡專案。市場上多項 AI 相關 GPU/CPU/ASIC 專案交
錯推進需求快速成長且產品推出時程持續壓縮下，在供給成長速度仍受植針效率受限的情況下，更
多 AI 周邊晶片的客戶開始脫離既有供應商體係，尋求多家供應商負責同一專案以確保晶片能在客
戶指定時程內交付。精測在過往在 MEMS 探針擴產策略上較為保守，主因手機市場市佔率變動較
大。然在 EV AI 大案件確認將進入量產階段，預計公司將能利用自製植針機以及探針的優勢，將現
有每月 7-80 萬 MEMS 探針產能於 2Q27 擴充至每月 120-130 萬根針。在產能大幅擴充且雙晶片探
針卡技術已通過客戶認證之下，預計公司亦有機會切入該客戶採相似設計的其餘 AI CPU 探針卡案
件成為供應商之一。目前該客戶 CPU 案件仍在工程驗證階段，預計於 4Q26-1Q27 tape out ，CPU
探針卡量產營收貢獻則主要將落在 2H27。預計 2027 年受惠 EV AI 探針卡大案件進入量產出貨以
及 AI CPU 在 2H27 進入初期拉貨下，預計公司 HPC 探針卡營收將年增 282%，營收佔比由 8%提
升到 17%。 
此外，目前觀察到預燒爐客戶在美國 的產能爬坡進展順利，且除了既有 TPU 客戶外亦成功切入
GPU 客戶之下，預期 2027 年公司預燒爐測試板以及系統板相關營收將優於先前預期達到公司整體
營收 20%。同時，在公司持續轉嫁成本上漲以及高毛利 FT/CP 產品單價隨主要客戶下一代晶片規
格一同成長帶動之下，預期 2027 年毛利率將能維持在 55%，達公司指引區間的上緣，並帶動獲利
率年增 4 個百分點至 37.5，EPS 年增 95%至 120 元。

### Page 4

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：半導體 2026 年 9 月 10 日 Chunghwa Precision Test 第4 頁，共 13頁  
產業概況  
探針卡產值將以 CAGR 12%成長至 2027 年 
晶圓測試(CP)的主要目的是為了在將晶圓切割成晶片並進入封裝流程前，檢測其上是否存在瑕疵，
以節省封裝有瑕疵裸晶造成的多餘成本，同時監控製造良率。 CP 測試項目通常包含直流電 (DC)測
試，該測試會評估半導體設備的基本電性，如電壓、電流和電阻。晶圓測試 (CP)的測試介面採用探
針卡，本中心預估 2024-2027 年探針卡產值將以 CAGR 12%成長。 
圖 5：半導體測試流程主要分為前段(CP)及後段(FT)

資料來源：公司資料、元大投顧預估 
此外，晶片在封裝後需要再經過後段測試(FT)，而有些高功率晶片還會經過老化測試(Burn-in Test) 。
近期，本中心觀察到旗艦 AP 及 AI GPU 已開始引進系統級測試(SLT)。隨著測試時間增加，本中心
預估測試座(Test Socket) 產值年複合成長率將達 10%，優於老化測試座之 5%，而整體測試座產值
將以 CAGR 8%成長至 2026 年。 
圖 6：探針卡產值將以 CAGR 12% 成長至
2027 年 
圖 7：測試座產值將以 CAGR 8%成長至 2027 年

獲利調整與股票評價  
探針產能擴充及通過驗證的技術將有助於切入客戶其餘案件並帶動評價提升  
目前公司已通過 EV AI 探針卡客戶驗證，預計在 1H27 將開始出貨量產探針卡予客戶。此案件預計
在 2027 年將出貨約 100 張探針卡，佔營收約 8%，成為公司最大的單一探針卡專案。在 EV AI 大
案件確認將進入量產階段，公司預計將現有每月 7-80 萬 MEMS 探針產能於 2Q27 擴充至每月 120-
130 萬根針。且在公司雙晶片探針卡技術已通過客戶認證之下，預計公司將有更多機會切入該客戶
採相似設計其餘 AI CPU 探針卡案件成為供應商之一。此外，預燒爐客戶產能爬坡進展順利，2027
年預燒爐相關營收將年增 206%達到 20%的營收佔比。 
公司目前交易於 30 倍 2027 年本益比，低於國內同業的 36 倍，然在探針卡有望透過已經完成驗證
的技術切入客戶其餘 AI CPU 探針卡案件且 HPC 測試板業務預計將在 2026-27 以 110%的強勁成
長趨勢不變下，基於切入 AI 供應鏈後的約 1x 標準差本益比 35x 與 2027F EPS NT$120 ，給予目
標價 4250 元的買進評等。
